### 164. Acknowledgment amendment acceptance UX (counter-acceptance flow)
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11.6). EA amendments converted from unilateral grantor mutations to bilateral proposals requiring grantee acceptance. Closes the post-acceptance acknowledgment-injection exploit. Three new factory helpers (`proposeEvaluationAgreementAmendment`, `acceptEvaluationAgreementAmendment`, `rejectEvaluationAgreementAmendment`) replace the unilateral `makeAmendedEvaluationAgreement`. New EA `status` enum value `'pending-acceptance'` between `'active'` and a possible return to `'active'`. New per-amendment shape with `id` / `status` / `proposalMessage` / `responseMessage` / `responseDate` / `termsBefore` / `proposed` / `acknowledgmentChanges`. New `AmendmentResponseModal` for the grantee-side accept/reject UX with per-row acknowledgment ticking + expiration confirm. Three new notification types `v22-ea-amendment-proposal` / `v22-ea-amendment-accepted` / `v22-ea-amendment-rejected` (sunset the old `v22-ea-amendment` informational type). Pending-acceptance lock blocks Run Evaluation submit + Amend EA button on both grantor and grantee sides; revoke is the only override. Scope was deliberately limited to EA amendments — DA amendments stay unilateral pending future work tracked under #167. Architecture spec §11.2a major revision documents the new lifecycle + record shape; new §11.2b documents the lock semantics; §7.4 replaces the old single notification row with three new rows.

### 102. Disclosure amendment notifications missing on counterparty side
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11E.2 + 11E.4 rollback). DA-amendment scope only — the parallel Claim-amendment fan-out shipped briefly in Phase 11E.2 and was rolled back in Phase 11E.4 after a conceptual review concluded that grantees only learn of new Claim content via Disclosure Agreement amendment, never via the Claim amendment itself. Two preserved deliverables: (a) the existing DA-amendment notification was renamed `v22-amendment` → `v22-da-amendment` (parallel to `v22-ea-amendment`), with click handler updated to deep-link directly to the DA Detail Panel via `setOpenAgreement({ kind: 'disclosure', disclosureAgreementId })`. Badge label initially `DA AMENDED` (Phase 11E.2), then unified to `AMENDMENT` (Phase 11E.4) when the EA amendment badge was unified to the same label. (b) Notification body copy now includes the Claim name + optional `(Note: …)` suffix — pre-fix the body fell through to a bare `req.asset?.name`. Architecture spec §7.4 notification table extended with `v22-da-amendment` row; §11.2 prototype notes updated for the DA-amendment case; §6 worked example + §11.3 evaluation-amendment narrative now reference the DA-amendment type.

### 43. Clickable Directory Layer dots
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 16.0). Each Claim dot is hoverable (whitens on direct hover, owning cluster brightens via `filter: brightness(1.18)`) and clickable (pins the tooltip card + opens the Detail Panel via `onClaimDotClick`). Background click un-pins and dismisses the panel. Tooltip card mirrors the parent-layer dot-preview pattern (CLAIM badge + name + owner + disclosure type + posted date). RFP dots ship as non-functional placeholders for Phase 17.

### 45. Real dot-cloud data sourcing
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 16.0). Cluster contents derive from per-role disclosure visibility via the new `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole(roleId, provisionals)` helper in `v2_2Data.js`. Public DAs (subject kind=claim, grantee=Radiant Network) → indigo dots cross-Actor. Umbrella DAs (subject kind=claim, grantee=active party, grantor=non-active non-network party) → amber dots in the active Actor's view. Public-and-umbrella Claims render indigo per spec §8.2.2. Mock supplier clusters (NovaFab, ElectroGrid, Precision Components) and the standalone Phase 11A ChipCo cluster removed; the new model derives clusters from real seed data. Phase 16 ships limited density (Bob + Dave's umbrella relationship + Alice's three publics + Carol auditor view); future Phase 16.1+ may add force-directed or stratified layout at scale.

### Visual & Rendering — completed

### 176c. Chip container visual tuning — shield halo, overlap cut, vertical centering, single-shield centering
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 14.5). Each shield carries a 2px halo stroked in the rectangle's exact background color (`color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent-indigo) 18%, var(--bg-card))`); when adjacent shields overlap, the halo paints negative space between them — the classic overlapping-tokens cut. Shield size 20 → 18px; STEP_IDLE 15 → 12 (~33% overlap); STEP_FAN 24 → 22 (= 18 + 4 gap); container height 26 → 24. Shields vertically centered via `top: (HEIGHT - SHIELD_SIZE) / 2`. Single-shield case renders a 24×24 square with the lone shield centered both horizontally and vertically; multi-shield right-anchored layout unchanged. `BadgeShieldIcon` gained an optional `strokeColor` prop with `overflow: visible` SVG so the halo doesn't clip the viewBox.

### 176b. Chip container visual polish — drop wrappers, scale up shields
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 14.4). Removed the per-shield circular wrapper (was `borderRadius: 50%` with `var(--bg-card)` background + 1.5px indigo border + shadow) and the "+N" pill wrapper (was 8px-radius indigo-tinted pill with border + shadow). `BadgeShieldIcon` SVG now renders directly inside the chip rectangle; "+N" renders as plain indigo monospaced text. Shields scaled 16px → 20px to fill the freed space; container height adjusts 22px → 26px. STEP values rescaled proportionally to maintain the 25%-overlap idle and 4px-gap fan-out relationships. Container background, border, shadow, fan-out animation timing, and tooltip behavior unchanged.

### 176a. Badge chip container refinement with hover fan-out + tooltips
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 14.3). Replaced the Phase 14.1 pattern of independent absolute-positioned chips + a separate "+N" pill with a single rounded-rectangle container matching the NEW pill's visual treatment (4px border-radius, indigo-tinted background, subtle shadow). Hover fan-out (when 2+ shields): rectangle expands leftward via 180ms ease-out; rightmost shield + "+N" stay anchored, previously-overlapped shields slide left to un-overlap with 4px spacing. Per-shield tooltip reveals badge name + version (line 1) + issuer party (line 2); "+N" tooltip lists buried badges as `Badge Name · Issuer Party` rows. Both use the shared `<Tooltip>` primitive with auto-flip below the anchor when insufficient vertical space above. Click guard via `e.stopPropagation()` so chip clicks don't trigger card-level interactions. New module `src/v2/BadgeChipContainer.jsx` extracted from AssetNode.jsx; `_activeBadges` decoration in V2App enriched with template name + version for tooltip rendering.

### 9. Richer provisional → active transition
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11C.3–11C.5 wired the flip-from-provisional → active card animation; verified Phase 12.2). Animation primitive at `src/v2/animations/reveal.js`. Pairs with #139 (Phase 11E.3 ship). Moved to Completed Phase 12.2 — sufficient for current bar.

### 110. Edge glow + marching-ants animations (V2.1 restoration)
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (shipped during V2.2 polish work; verified Phase 12.2). Edge styling palette in `V2Canvas.jsx` carries the dashed/solid + opacity pattern from V2.1 — visually equivalent. Moved to Completed Phase 12.2 — already shipped during V2.2 polish work.

### 44. Radiant Network Actor node on owner canvas
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11A seeded the node via `buildViewForActor` line 1725 — `if (hasPublished) actors.push(RADIANT_NETWORK_ACTOR)`; Phase 11.8 added the double-click interaction). Double-clicking the Radiant Network Actor card opens the Public Directory with the circular wipe originating from the card's screen-space center instead of the chrome globe-button corner. New `wipeOrigin` prop on `DirectoryLayer` accepts a viewport-pixel `{ x, y }`; the open-phase pin captures the origin so the close animation collapses back to the same point. Globe-button click path is unchanged (passes null → falls back to bottom-left corner).

### 165. Smooth out edge draw-in animation
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11.6). `addRevealOverlayEdge` in V2Canvas.jsx now forces `pointCount = 64` for reveal overlay edges (was 12/20/32 from canonical buildEdges curvature heuristic). Smaller per-frame `instanceCount` deltas eliminate the "steppy" quantization that was visible during the 1.2s draw-in window — 64 segments at ~60fps gives ~1 segment per frame. Geometry cost is bounded (one overlay edge per reveal, ~2s lifetime). Canonical buildEdges resolution unchanged.

### 139. Edge geometry animation during reveal flip
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11E.3). New animation primitive `src/v2/animations/edgeDrawIn.js` exports `playEdgeDrawIn({ nodeId, canvasRef, durationMs })`. New `playEdgeDrawIn(nodeId, durationMs = 500)` method on V2Canvas mirrors `playEdgeRetract` in reverse: per-frame point-trim grows the visible curve from a 2-point stub at the anchor to full length over `durationMs`, with material opacity ramping 0 → base over the first 30% so the edge fades in at the head of the draw. `startReveal` in V2App.jsx schedules the draw-in at `setTimeout(500ms)` so it fires after the reveal pan settles and completes before the flip starts (`PHASE_FLIP_MS = 1100`). The edge's `_showAsProvisional` stamp clears at flip-midpoint via the existing reveal infrastructure, so the dashed-grey provisional edge becomes its final typed style at the visual hand-off — capturing the spirit of "typed edge draws in" using the existing single-edge-with-stamp architecture rather than maintaining two parallel edges.

### 1. Warmer grey border on all nodes
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A). `warmBorder = color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent-indigo) 22%, var(--border))` — reads as a cool indigo-grey that stops node terminations from fading into the dark canvas without competing with indigo edges. Red UNSAT border treatment unchanged.

### 2. Visual distinction for counterparty-pulled-in nodes
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A). Counterparty cards (where `node.owner !== activeParty`, excluding Actor nodes) render a muted tint: `color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-card) 82%, var(--bg-deep))`. Subtle flattening, no opacity/chip changes.

### 3. Subtle de-emphasis for internal/ownership edges
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A). Internal edges (where `edge.grantorParty === edge.granteeParty`, carried through from `deriveAgreementEdges`) now render at 70% of the default stroke width. Selected and NEW edges keep their emphasis regardless.

### 5. Node label truncation legibility
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A, expanded). Claim and Eval Result names wrap to two lines via `-webkit-line-clamp: 2`; Actor and Asset names stay on one line with ellipsis. Also in Phase 9A: vertical spacing above the name increased so the `CLAIM` / `EVAL RESULT` type badge no longer crowds the name, and the health minibar's wrapper flex-space-between-s the inner content so the whitespace between the owner row and the card edge equalises.

### 40. Node card action button reassessment post-migration
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A). V2.2 nodes route through a new `V22ActionBar` component that mirrors the Detail Panel footer one-to-one per type: ASSET (owner) → Request Agreement + Parse Evidence + Create Claim; CLAIM (owner) → Amend Claim + Self-Evaluate; CLAIM (non-owner + active EA) → Run Evaluation; EVAL RESULT (owner, not superseded) → Re-run Evaluation; PARSE RESULT / ACTOR → none. Single dispatch prop `onV22CardAction(actionName, node)` routes from card click → V2Canvas → V2App's action handlers, the same handlers V22NodeDetailPanel's footer fires. Legacy V2.1 ActionBar is retained as fallback for non-V2.2 nodes.

### 52. "Human-validated" indicator on Eval/Parse review rows
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A item 10). `_aiOriginalValue` snapshot tracked per row from initialization (set to the AI's extracted value, or empty string for fresh rows). When `row.value !== row._aiOriginalValue` the modal + Detail Panel render a small amber pencil SVG next to the ConfidenceBadge with the tooltip "Human-edited from AI's original extraction." Persisted onto the submitted Parse Result `fields` and Eval Result `results` so the Detail Panel can render the pencil later. AI confidence remains unchanged by human edits (the Phase 8.5 rule).

### 60. Dot-LOD alignment with background dot matrix
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9E-parallel initial approach → 9E-parallel.1 correction, commit 7d03982). Initial 9E-parallel approach lowered background opacity at base depth — wrong direction per Andrew's feedback: background matrix is intentional visual infrastructure and should stay at full brightness. 9E-parallel.1 corrected: restored uniform background opacity across all depths (0.28 dark / 0.32 light); brightened AssetNodeDot inner-circle ring stroke 1px → 1.5px and color `WARM_BORDER` (40% indigo blend) → `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent-indigo) 70%, var(--border))`. Grid alignment was already in place via the existing `snapToGrid` function — no alignment work required. Contrast between nodes and background is now carried by the node dot's ring, not by dimming the background.

### 63. Mini/dot LOD warmer borders
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A.1.5). `WARM_BORDER` (40% indigo blend) extended to mini and dot LOD renderings — mini cards now match full cards; dots grow a 1px indigo ring so they don't fade into canvas at zoom-out. Red UNSAT borders unchanged.

### 100. Mini/dot LOD action buttons on hover
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (9A.6.1; reverted to click-only behavior in 9A.6.1.1 — hover-to-show was visually present but impractical as the pointer couldn't reach the buttons before they dismissed). Action bar renders at mini/dot LODs on node selection, matching full-card LOD behavior. `onV22CardAction` threading through AssetNodeMini + AssetNodeDot from 9A.6.1 is retained — that plumbing remains needed for click-selected action bar dispatch. The `forceActionBar` prop on AssetNode was removed.

### 107. Border shorthand vs. longhand style warning
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9E-parallel, commit b29fdc9). Original diagnosis was "shorthand `border:` paired with a side-specific longhand like `borderTopColor`." Code audit during 9E-parallel found no such pattern in AssetNode.jsx. **Actual root cause:** React's style reconciler has trouble tracking `border-color` transitions when the border is set via shorthand `border: ...`. Fix: convert every shorthand paired with a `transition: border-color` to longhand (`borderWidth` + `borderStyle` + `borderColor`). Four call sites fixed in AssetNode.jsx: full-card selection border, full-card main div, mini selection border, mini main div. Dot-card borders and other static non-transitioning borders left as shorthand.

### 124. Revoked node unravel animation sequence
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9D.2; revisited in Phase 9D.2.1 to ship the proper staged choreography + revoked-edge persistence + visibility-based pan skip; further refined in 9D.2.2 / 9D.2.3 / 9D.2.4 / 9D.4.1).
- **Phase 9D.2 (initial ship, 2026-04-26):** `src/v2/animations/unravel.js` exports `playUnravelAnimation` returning a Promise. Stages: (0) pan/zoom to node, (1) Three.js edge retract, (2-4) CSS card unravel. New V2Canvas methods: `getNodeWorldPos`, `isFocusedOnPoint`, `playEdgeRetract`. New `v22UnravelingNodeId` state in V2App. Wired into `handleV22DismissRevoked` + `handleV22DismissOrphanedEvalResult` — both `async` and `await` the primitive before mutating state.
- **Phase 9D.2.1 (revision, 2026-04-27):** revoked DAs/EAs persist as styled edges through Dismiss; new `isNodeVisibleInViewport` accounts for Detail Panel offset; three layered keyframes replace the single coordinated keyframe (border erosion + per-row content fade + card-level fade).
- **Phase 9D.2.2 (corrections, 2026-04-27):** revoked-edge color persistence in `applyEdgeStylingRef`; `unravelingRef` suppresses selection-pan effect; `SLOW_MODE_MULTIPLIER` constant for QA.
- **Phase 9D.2.3 (refinements, 2026-04-27):** point-trim edge retract (was lerp); deselect + panel close before unravel; clip-path right-to-left text wipe.
- **Phase 9D.2.4 (correction, 2026-04-27):** suppress edge rebuild during the unravel via `unravelingRef.current` early-return on the edge-rebuild useEffect.

### 127. Tooltip arrow alignment
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9D.1.2 W2). Tooltip arrow sat ~ARROW_SIZE px off-center on the Agreements Section Amend / Revoke rows. ### Edge Interactions — completed

### 203. Rare edge-tooltip stickiness across layer transition (Round 19 residual)
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 18.0.1, 2026-05-21). Filed intermittent in Phase 17.4.4; became reliably reproducible during the RFP-walkthrough demo recording (the parent-canvas "SELECT EDGE TO VIEW" hover tooltip persisted over the Directory layer). **Root cause:** V2Canvas's `onMouseMove`-bound `handleEdgeHover` had no Directory-open guard — only the existing `transitioningRef.current` check (V2Canvas-internal dive/surface transitions). 17.4.3's `clearEdgeUiState` cleared all three edge-tooltip state layers correctly + synchronously, but afterward any mousemove during/after the ~600ms Directory wipe re-raycasted a parent edge, re-fired `onEdgeHover`, and re-set V2App's `edgeHover` → the `EdgeHoverMenu` re-rendered (z-index 6000, portaled to document.body) over the Directory. The "intermittent" framing was because repro needed the cursor over a hit-range edge + any mousemove post-clear — both common during recording. **Fix:** (1) new `directoryOpen` prop on V2Canvas with early-return guards in `handleEdgeHover` + `handleBackgroundClick`, plus a `useEffect` that clears V2Canvas's internal hover state + cancels the 150ms debounce on the `directoryOpen` false→true flip; (2) V2App passes `directoryOpen={v22DirectoryOpen}`, gates both `EdgeHoverMenu` mounts on `!v22DirectoryOpen` (defensive backstop), and adds `setEdgeMenuPanning(false)` to `clearEdgeUiState` for pinned-state completeness. Build clean; directory open/close cycle verified clean with no console errors (~900 parent-canvas mousemoves while Directory open produced no tooltip ghost). The hover-tooltip repro itself is canvas-raycaster-gated (DOM-dispatched mousemoves don't trigger the Three.js edge raycaster, per the documented limitation) — verified via the guard path + code review + clean open/close cycle.

### 35. Edge-draw animation for new Amend Claim references
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 18.0, 2026-05-21). `makeAmendedClaim` in `v2_2Data.js` stamps `_isNew: true` + a numeric `_createdAt` on the new claim-ref DAs it builds for added (and supersede-target) Asset references. `deriveAgreementEdges`'s `pushEdge` propagates both flags from the source DA onto the derived edge, so V2Canvas's existing edge-draw animation (which zeroes + grows a `_isNew` Line2's `instanceCount`, gated to a 3s `_createdAt` window) animates the new Claim ↔ Asset edges in — matching the post-disclosure-accept reveal. Because claim-ref DAs are *permanent* internal DAs (not transient provisionals), the amend handler (`handleV22AmendClaimSubmit`) clears the reveal flags ~1.1s after the draw-in (scoped to the just-added DA ids) — otherwise the edge would keep rendering with `_isNew`'s brighter/thicker styling. No cross-impact on the provisional `e-prov-` edge reveal path. The animation itself is rAF-paced (manual-verify under headless preview's rAF throttling); the data/propagation path + build are verified.

Old `EdgeMenu.jsx` deleted.

### 116. Agreements section on Eval Result + Parse Result Detail Panels
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 18.0, 2026-05-21). Extended the Phase 9C `AgreementsSection` (Actor / Asset / Claim) to **V22EvalResultPanel** + **V22ParseResultPanel**. V2App's parent-canvas Detail Panel mount gained two new agreement-filter branches: Eval Result → `disclosureAgreementsForNode = allDas.filter(d => d.subject?.kind === 'evalResult' && d.subject.id === node.id)` (catches the evaluator's auto-disclosure / Proof-of-Evaluation DA flowing to the Claim owner + any further disclosures); Parse Result → `subject.kind === 'parseResult'` (yields empty in V2.2 — Parse Results flow through their parent Asset's disclosure — so the section renders nothing, matching 9C's `return null` empty convention). Subject name displays the eval-result id (the shared `resolveSubjectName` resolver returns `artifact.name`, which eval-result artifacts don't carry — pre-existing resolver behavior, out of this phase's no-new-primitives scope).

### 19. Published standards data

### 122. Remove evidence from a Claim despite prior evaluation (Asset versioning + OUTDATED state)
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.2). Claims gain a parallel `assetReferences[]` audit chain on top of the existing primitive `referencedAssetIds[]` (the active in-scope set). Each chain entry: `{ assetId, supersededBy, addedDate, removedDate }`. AmendClaim grew Replace + Remove affordances per evaluated-Asset row — Replace opens a successor picker that stamps `supersededBy` on the existing entry and appends a fresh entry for the replacement; Remove stamps `removedDate` on the existing entry without successor. The Asset node itself is NEVER modified. New Eval Result `status` value `'outdated'` triggered when any `evidenceUsed` Asset has been superseded or removed since the evaluation. `isEvalResultStale(evalResult, claim)` walks the chain to detect; AmendClaim submit fires `v22-eval-result-stale` notifications (single-grantee, informational, click deep-links to the Eval Result) and flips status synchronously. AssetNode renders an OUTDATED badge in Row 0 + dashed amber card border. Eval Result Detail Panel renders an "Out of date" notice section near the top with re-run guidance. Re-run produces a new active Eval Result via #117's flow; the prior OUTDATED Eval Result transitions to `'superseded'`. New helper `getLatestAssetVersion(assetId, claim)` mirrors `getLatestRSVersion` from #120. Documented deviation: existing 42+ call sites read `referencedAssetIds` as primitives — kept that shape and added the chain in parallel rather than converting all consumers; the brief explicitly allowed this either-or path.

### 120. Reference published Requirements Sets on a Claim (non-binding)
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.1). Claims gain `referencedRequirementsSets[]` of `{ requirementsSetId, addedDate }` entries. **Informational only** — does not couple to evaluation, does not auto-suggest in Run Evaluation, does not produce notifications when changed. **Owner-authoritative** — only the Claim owner can edit; references do not inherit from Assets. **Pool:** owner's authored RS + publicly published RS; privately-shared RS deferred. **Edit pathways:** Create-time picker + AmendClaim add/remove (cascade-skip — no Eval Result staleness, no notifications) + inline `UpdateRSReferenceModal` for single-row supersession updates (the only inline mutation affordance on this section, justified because version drift is passive state). **Version-pinned** — references store the specific version id; library supersession surfaces a "Newer version available" pill rather than auto-retargeting. **Mutable lifecycle** — no lock-on-disclosure semantics. New shared `RequirementsSetPicker` primitive used by Create + Amend Claim flows. Demo data: Alice and Dave's seeded Claims now reference public + authored standards; MIL-PRF-55681 ships in v1 + v2 so the supersession surfacing is visible on first load. New helper `getLatestRSVersion(rsId, allRS)` walks the lineage chain. Architecture spec §10.3 updated + new §10.3a documenting the field semantics, version-pinning, and inline-mutation exception.

### 65. Credit charge for Asset registration + Claim creation
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 9A.6 Gate A). `CREDITS_PER_ASSET = 5`, `CREDITS_PER_CLAIM = 25` constants. `CreditCostRow` shared component. Submit disabled when under-funded.

### 70. Asset hierarchy — Asset-from-Asset registration
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 10.2). Decision (a) chosen — `parentAssetId` field on the Asset schema. Constraints: child Assets must share the parent's owner; cycles forbidden; counterparties never see hierarchy; Claims do NOT implicitly include child Assets. Layout uses depth-based elastic columns. Detail Panel surfaces clickable Parent + Children sections. Spec §3.2 + §10.1 + §6.4 updated.

### 119. "Evidence" → "Assets" terminology audit
- **Status:** ✅ Complete for the V22RunEvaluationModal call sites (Phase 11D W6). "Evidence in scope (N)" → "Assets in scope (N)"; "...without evidence (self-attestation)" → "...as a self-attestation"; "Select at least one evidence Asset" → "Select at least one Asset"; "(Requirements Set, evidence) combination" → "(Requirements Set, Asset selection) combination"; processing subtitle "across N evidence file(s)" → "across N Asset(s)". Internal variable names kept per the user-facing-vs-internal boundary. Pairs with the broader #17 client-canon reconciliation.

### Process Flows — completed

### 105. Run Evaluation modal: empty-evidence copy update
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.2). Owner viewing their own evidence-less Claim sees "There is no evidence associated with this Claim. Add evidence to self-evaluate." Non-owner (evaluator) sees "There is no evidence associated with this Claim. Ask the owner of this Claim to add evidence to evaluate." Both gate the modal's submit action — empty-evidence Claims cannot be evaluated. New `isOwnerView` prop on `V22RunEvaluationModal`; V2App resolves at mount time via `claim?.owner === activeRole.party`.

### 106. Remove evidence picker from Run Evaluation modal
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.2). Modal opens directly to a review-rows surface — no Asset picker step. `evidenceUsed` is computed at submit time as the snapshot of all currently-in-scope Asset references on the Claim (filtered through the supersession chain). Existing single-Asset-list summary rendering retained as a read-only "Assets in scope (N) — auto-snapshot at submit" panel; the legacy `evidenceSelection` state stays as a no-op placeholder so the processing-stage copy referencing the Asset count works unchanged. Submit gates simplified — empty-evidence Claims block via the empty-state copy path instead of the prior "select at least one Asset" affordance. Pairs with #88 (transfer cascade) — both deal with Claim-vs-evidence boundary semantics; this item consolidated the boundary on the evaluation side.

### 117. Re-Run Evaluation: diff readout
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.2; reshaped from "permissive Asset selection"). Re-run shows a "Changes since last evaluation" banner above the review rows in the Run Evaluation modal when a prior Eval Result by the same evaluator on this Claim has a non-empty diff vs. the current effective evidence snapshot. Banner counts: +N Assets, −M Assets, S superseded, K carried over. Banner suppressed when no prior or fully empty diff. Resulting Eval Result Detail Panel renders a "Changes from prior evaluation" section with the same diff content, persistent in audit trail. New helper `computeEvidenceDiff(priorEvalResult, claim)` returns `{ added, removed, superseded, carried }`. Re-run flips the prior Eval Result's status to `'superseded'` with `supersededBy` set to the new Eval Result's id; new Eval Result carries `priorEvalResultId` + `evidenceDiff` for audit. NEW badges on freshly-disclosed Assets (the brief's auxiliary item from prior framing) deferred — current diff banner readout covers the "what changed since" question for evaluators.

### 121. Evaluate a Claim against multiple Requirements Sets simultaneously
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 12.2). Run Evaluation modal's RS picker grew a primary/additional split: clicking a row sets it as the primary RS (whose review rows surface in step 2); clicking the `+ BATCH` chip toggles a row as a sibling (no separate review rows — uses AI confidence + status verbatim). Submit produces N Eval Results, all sharing a freshly-generated `batchId` (`batch-{timestamp}-{rand}`). Solo evaluations carry `batchId: null`. `makeEvaluationResult` extended with `batchId`, `priorEvalResultId`, `evidenceDiff` fields. V22EvalResultPanel renders a "Sibling Evaluations" section listing batch members when `batchId !== null` and the batch contains 2+ members; section omitted otherwise (backwards-compat for pre-12.2 seed Eval Results). Demo data: Bob's existing PRM Eval Result retro-fitted with `batchId: 'batch-seed-bob-prm-001'`; new sibling Eval Result `eval-bob-prm-002` against System Integration v1 ships in the same batch + new proof / ownership DAs so both batch members appear on Alice's canvas.

### 54. Total reset button in user menu
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11.8). New "Reset all data" action in the user menu dropdown, below the SWITCH USER block under its own divider. Click opens a confirmation modal explaining the scope (every role's canvas state, notifications, provisional artifacts) before committing. On confirm: `v22Provisionals` reset to empty initial shape; `perRoleState` reset to `emptyRoleState` for every role; `credits` reset to the active role's seeded balance; selection / Detail Panel / Directory state cleared. Theme + skip-boot localStorage flags are preserved (user preferences, not demo state). Note the original brief mentioned "clears localStorage and re-plays the boot sequence" — that path was deemed too aggressive for the demo (theme regression + forced 3D boot every reset), so the shipped scope is in-memory state only. Pairs with #53 (session persistence) which would extend the reset surface to persisted state when that ships.

### 98. Credit warning copy + add-credits modal link
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11.8). `CreditCostRow` (`src/components/modals/ModalShared.jsx`) drops the "Only" prefix on insufficient — both states show plain "N available" with sufficient styled neutrally and insufficient styled red. New `onAddCreditsClick` prop renders an "Add credits →" link to the right of the available count when wired (indigo, dashed underline). V22CreateAssetModal + V22CreateClaimModal both forward the prop; nested register-from-Claim flow forwards through. V2App opens a sub-modal (`v22AddCreditsOpen`) on top of the parent Create modal with two actions: "+100 credits" (accent indigo) and "Reset to role default". Both close only the sub-modal so the parent stays open and the user sees the updated balance reflected immediately in the CREDIT COST row.

### 99. Create Claim picker: pre-selected + newly-registered Assets at top with NEW badges
- **Status:** ✅ Complete (Phase 11.8). `V22CreateClaimModal` Asset picker now floats `initialAssetIds` + Assets registered via the inline "+ Register new Asset…" CTA to the top of the list, each with an indigo NEW badge (uppercase mono, dashed-blend background). Tracking via two Sets: `recentlyRegisteredIds` (seeded with `initialAssetIds` + grown by `handleNestedAssetComplete`) and `clearedBadgeIds` (grown when the user toggles a NEW row from selected → unselected). The badge clears on first deselect and does not return on re-selection — the badge answers "what's new since I opened this modal" rather than persisting for the modal's lifetime. Sort is stable: NEW rows before others, otherwise original `ownedAssets` order preserved. No section header or visual separator; the badge alone marks the boundary.

### 33. Transferring process (ownership transfer)

Provisional transfer lands on `v22Provisionals.transfers`; sender's Asset gets TRANSFERRING badge. Recipient's Accept flips ownership and appends to `dot.lineage[]`. Decline appends declined record without changing ownership.

Wires ONE qualified-storage file (`emi-shield-spec.pdf`) end-to-end so it previews live in the picker AND in the resulting registered Asset's File Viewer; the other five MicroCo datasheets stay preview-less placeholders by design. **(File 1)** the attached `emi-shield-spec.pdf` (4-page MicroCo-branded spec, ~10 KB) lives at `public/seed-pdfs/emi-shield-spec.pdf` (alongside the Phase 15 branded PDFs). **(File 2 — `V22QualifiedStoragePicker.jsx`)** the MicroCo `/product-data/datasheets` mock entry for `emi-shield-spec.pdf` gains `localPath: '/seed-pdfs/emi-shield-spec.pdf'` (field name matches the existing branded-PDF shape that `makeAsset` reads). **(File 3 — same file)** imported the Phase 15 `AnnotatedPdfViewer`; `selectedFileToV22Payload` now carries `localPath` (else `null`); the single-file File Details preview slot renders `<AnnotatedPdfViewer fileUrl={previewFile.localPath} height="100%" />` when `previewFile.localPath` is set, else the existing "Preview not available" placeholder. **(Files 4 + 5 — NO code change needed):** the picker payload's `localPath` flows unchanged through `V22CreateAssetModal` (`{ ...r.file }` spread) → `handleV22CreateAssetSubmit` (`row.file`) → `makeAssetRegistrationArtifacts` → `makeAsset`, which ALREADY maps `file.localPath` + `file.mimeType` (lines 289/284). So the registered Asset carries the PDF forward automatically, and the existing `AssetFileViewer` iframe rail renders it — identical to how the seeded branded-PDF Assets (Alice's PRM Datasheet etc.) already render. `mimeType` resolves to `application/pdf` from the `.pdf` ext via the picker's existing `MIME_BY_EXT`. **(File 6)** footer `v0.20.3 → v0.20.4` + Changelog modal entry. **Runtime status (build clean — 132 modules, 0 errors):** **live-verified end-to-end** as Alice — QA #1 (picker File Details renders the PDF via PDF.js: 4 page canvases, "Preview not available" gone); QA #2 (content readable: MICROCO branding, "EMI Shield Specification" title, "PRM-3A Power Regulation Module" subtitle, Mechanical Specifications + Electromagnetic Performance tables — confirmed by screenshot); QA #3 (registered the Asset → its File Viewer opened with iframe `src="/seed-pdfs/emi-shield-spec.pdf"`, metadata Filename/Size 1.40 MB/MIME application/pdf, no "No preview available"); QA #5 (clicked powerregulationmodule-datasheet → "Preview not available", 0 PDF canvases); QA #7 (PDF fetches 200 OK, `content-type: application/pdf`, `%PDF-` magic, 10709 bytes). Footer rolls forward to v0.20.4.
- 2026-05-27: **Phase 20.3 — Login screen lightning color randomization.** Standalone demo-prep visual polish (not a tracked backlog item — surfaced during Phase 20.2 walkthrough prep); single file `src/v2/V2BootScreen.jsx` + footer/Changelog. The login-phase lightning bolts previously all drew toward one gold tone; now each bolt picks one of five equal-weighted colors at spawn and holds it for its lifetime, with dots inheriting the color of the brightest bolt that touched them. **(Palette resolution — Option α):** at canvas init, `getComputedStyle(document.documentElement)` reads `--accent-indigo` / `--accent-amber` / `--accent-green` / `--text-dim` from the live design tokens (parsed from `#RRGGBB` by a small inline hex parser — confirmed the tokens are clean 6-digit hex, no `rgb()`/other format — with hardcoded dark-theme fallbacks), plus hardcoded white, into a `PALETTE` array of five `{r,g,b}` triplets. **(Spawn pick):** `spawnBolt` adds `color: PALETTE[random]` to the pushed bolt. **(Per-dot propagation):** the brightness-update loop's existing `if (fadeIn > dot.brightness)` "brighter bolt wins" guard now also sets `dot.color = bolt.color` — stable per chain, brighter overlapping bolt overrides. **(Draw substitution):** the login-phase bright-dot branch interpolates from `normR/G/B` toward `dot.color || {gold}` instead of the hardcoded gold. **Boot-phase gold ripple unchanged** — `goldR/G/B` stay defined and still drive the post-Establish-Session network-build ripple (lines ~327-336). **Runtime status (build clean — 132 modules, 0 errors; preview boots into the login screen, no console errors):** PALETTE verified via live eval to resolve to the 5 correct colors (indigo 129/140/248, amber 245/158/11, green 34/197/94, grey 122/131/153, white 255/255/255; `allParsed: true`). **Canvas pixel-sampling confirmed the randomization renders:** a live `getImageData` scan found indigo (blue-dominant) lit pixels — which the old gold-only version could never produce (gold is near-zero blue) — alongside amber, grey, and white; two samples taken seconds apart showed the color distribution shifting frame-to-frame (sample 1: 38 indigo / 49 amber / 17 grey; sample 2: 0 indigo / 181 amber / 215 grey), proving per-bolt color variation over time. The chain now reads as a "hump" — diagonal down from the data row, horizontal across the eval band, diagonal back up to the counterparty — instead of piling onto Y=0 where edges overlapped the horizontal traffic lane. **All changes in `src/v2/v2_2Data.js`'s `buildV22Canvas` chain-Y assignment:** (A) new `EVAL_BAND_OFFSET = 300` constant near `ROW_STEP`/`COL_Y_OFFSET`; Pass 1 (anchor-Y inherit) sets `chainY = anchorY + EVAL_BAND_OFFSET` and tracks the offset Y in `usedErYs`; Pass 2 (collision search) bases at `anchorY + EVAL_BAND_OFFSET` (orphan fallback adds it on top of `COL_Y_OFFSET`); the Phase 11D.4.1 proof-only-pulled ER offset (`sourceClaim.y + 300`) now sources the constant; and every independent-Y eval-chain fallback (the ER defensive fallback + the three PoE fallbacks that could otherwise land at/near Y=0) carries the band offset. (B) the Pass 2 collision search biases away from center (steps further below first, only reaching back toward center when no slot is free) so additional chains stack below rather than crisscrossing the data row. PoE happy-path placement is unchanged in mechanism — it inherits the wrapped Eval Result's (now banded) Y automatically. No rendering changes (V2Canvas renders whatever Y it's given); seeded scenes AND newly-created Eval Results from `Run Evaluation` pick up the band automatically. **Runtime status (build clean — 132 modules, 0 errors; app boots clean, footer reads v0.19.0; only the pre-existing benign `NaN`-CSS-`left` edge-menu transient in console):** **data-probe-verified** via `buildV22Canvas(getV22DataForRole(roleId, null))` in the live dev server — **Bob:** PRM Claim Y=0 → its Eval Result Y=300 → PoE Y=300 (Avionics Asset Y=0); Voltage Regulator Claim Y=300 → its Eval Result Y=600 — eval-chain artifacts 300 below their anchor Claim (QA #1/#2/#3). **Alice:** Claims on the data row; her 4 pulled Eval Results at distinct Ys [300, 900, 600, 0] + 2 PoEs [300, 900], each 300 below its anchor Claim, no two sharing a Y (no crisscross — QA #4/#5); the Y=0 ER is the EMI chain whose anchor Claim is at Y=−300 (correctly +300 below its own anchor, different X column). **Carol:** two parallel chains at distinct Ys [300, 600], both humped (QA #6). **Dave:** fanout (Claims/Assets/Parse) unchanged; his single proof-only-pulled ER at Y=300 — identical to pre-Phase-19 (constant rename, QA #7/#10). - 2026-05-24: **Phase 18.3.0.2 — Self-eval orphan check: replace `evaluatorParty` signal with synthetic-EA id prefix.** Single-block predicate change at `V2App.jsx:9300–9327` (the `isOrphaned` Eval Result check). Phase 18.3.0.1 applied the self-eval orphan bypass correctly but used a signal that never fired: `er.evaluatorParty === claim.owner`. `evaluatorParty` is passed *into* `makeEvaluationRunArtifacts` (V2App:1956) as config, but the factory persists it as `owner`/`evaluator` on the output Eval Result artifact — there is **no** `er.evaluatorParty` field, and no `er.evaluatorParty` read sites exist anywhere in V2.2 (only `node.evaluatorParty`, an adapter-computed card-node property). So `er.evaluatorParty === undefined`, the bypass returned false for every self-eval result, and the predicate fell through to the EA lookup, which (correctly per architecture) never finds the synthetic, unpersisted self-eval EA → false "Orphaned Evaluation Result" banner. **Fix:** switch the signal to the synthetic-EA id prefix `er.evaluationAgreementId.startsWith('self-eval-')`. The synthetic self-eval EA's id is built at V2App:1913 as `` `self-eval-${claimId}` `` and is the sole site in the codebase emitting that prefix (verified by grep across `src/`); Andrew's orphan-banner screenshot literally shows the agreement id as `self-eval-claim-microco-rf-filter`. The check reads the same `evaluationAgreementId` field the EA lookup already uses, so the field is guaranteed populated. Strict relaxation — cross-party orphan cases (e.g. revoking Carol's EA on an Alice Claim) still flag orphan + present Dismiss-only; only self-eval is bypassed. Restores Re-Run + Create PoE to the Eval Result footer (footer↔action-bar parity) with no V22NodeDetailPanel edit (the footer falls through past the Dismiss-only early return into the existing Re-Run + Create PoE branch). **Verified:** `git diff` confirms the `evaluatorParty === claim.owner` check (and its `const claim = …find…` lookup) removed and `if (er.evaluationAgreementId.startsWith('self-eval-')) return false` added between the superseded check and the EA lookup; build clean. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.3.0.2.

The prompt's view-builder *override* (A2) + directed-DA *cluster pass* (A3) turned out to be **already delivered by existing infrastructure** — a solicitation DA is structurally a directed claim DA (grantee = owner, grantor = non-owner non-network, subject = claim), identical to a Phase 16.0/17.4 umbrella DA, so `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`'s umbrella pass already pulls the offered Claim into the solicitor's cluster (upserting if needed) and `buildViewForActor`'s party-scoping already suppresses the conflicting public DA (the owner never holds a Radiant-Network public DA). Added no redundant directed-DA passes (would double-add cluster dots); added a documenting comment at the umbrella pass instead. **(Part B — `SolicitationCreateModal.jsx`):** full restructure from a single-step Claim picker into a **4-step flow** — Step 1 Required Standards accordion + Claim picker (greying preserved), Step 2 Disclosure Type (3 DecisionCards, no Decline; Proof-Only disabled + tooltip when the Claim has no PoEs), Step 3 DA-expiry + branched scope picker (Full/Selective/Proof-Only) + Message (moved from Step 1), Step 4 Review. Mirrors CombinedResponseModal's `directoryPublishMode` primitives; DecisionCard/ExpiryPicker/StepDots from ModalShared; `isoFromPicker` + SdaLine + the 3-card set inlined locally (no cross-modal import). Submit emits `{ rfpId, claimId, message, disclosureType, scope, daExpiryIso }`. **(Part C — `V2App.jsx`):** `handleCreateSolicitation` mints the DA via `makeDisclosureAgreement` (grantor solicitor, grantee owner, subject claim, `_solicitationId`/`_rfpId` stamps), pushes it into `v22Provisionals` alongside the solicitation, and includes `claimId` in the notification; the modal mount pre-computes per-Claim `referencedAssetsByClaim`/`parseResultsByClaim`/`poesByClaim`; a shared `revokeSolicitationDa(daId, reason)` helper cascade-revokes the linked DA on reject (`handleRejectSolicitation`) and on RFP close (`handleCloseRfp` now rejects every open solicitation + revokes each DA — no double-notify, the v22-rfp-closed notification already informs solicitors). **Runtime-verified** (Alice/MicroCo soliciting Bob's Sentinel-4 RFP; build clean; only the pre-existing benign `NaN`-CSS-`left` edge-menu transient in console): the modal opens at **Step 1 of 4** (RFP Description + Required Standards (2) accordion + Claim picker with PRM Assembly / Voltage Regulator IC greyed "ON NETWORK", Continue gated), **Step 2 of 4** shows 3 type cards (no Decline), **Step 3 of 4** shows the DA-expiry picker (description names GovCo) + Asset list ("0 of 1") + Message textarea, **Step 4 of 4** Review shows For RFP / From MicroCo / To GovCo / Claim being offered EMI Shield Assembly / Disclosure Type Full + SdaLine / Scope 1 of 1 Asset / Disclosure Agreement Expires Never expires / Message — **no EA Expires, no Acknowledgments** — and "Submit Solicitation"; **Submit closes the modal and Alice's EMI Shield Claim panel shows a new "Full Disclosure · with GovCo" DA row** (atomic mint confirmed). Owner-side UI (RFP panel solicitation rows, Claim-panel solicitation-context actions, warm-path Request EA wiring, notification routing, Reject cascade UI) lands in **Phase 18.3.1**; the live owner-side Reject walkthrough (canvas-gated + the OLD reject UI 18.3.1 redesigns) is flagged for manual. The matching synthetic Claim card is stamped `_isNew` (NEW badge); a clear-on-deselect effect drops the flag when the selected Directory Claim is no longer the published one. **(Part B) Already-published gate:** `v22DataWithReveal` stamps `_publishedToDirectory: true` on owned Claim nodes whose Claim already has an active Radiant-Network-grantee DA (predicate shape mirrors §18.2.1.1). The V22ClaimPanel footer button + the V22ActionBar CLAIM-branch button both read the flag → disabled + explanatory tooltip; `ActionButton` gained `disabled` support (muted, `cursor: not-allowed`, inert onClick) for the action-bar parity. Flag clears reactively on revoke; a defensive no-op gate added to the `publishToDirectory` dispatch case. **(Part C) Parse Asset rename + iframe fallback:** modal title + entry-point labels rename "Parse Evidence" → "Parse Asset" (internal dispatch action `parseEvidence` unchanged); `AssetFileViewer` now guards the plain-iframe path — when `file.localPath` resolves to the SPA `index.html` fallback (missing physical PDF, detected on `onLoad` by sniffing the loaded doc for `#root`/the app title) or the iframe errors, it swaps to a "No preview available." placeholder instead of rendering the whole app inside the frame. **Runtime-verified** (Alice/MicroCo; build clean; zero console errors throughout): **Part A** — publishing the unpublished PCB Stackup Claim auto-transitions Parent → Directory and auto-opens its Detail Panel showing the new "with Radiant Network" DA row (the 60% camera landing + NEW-badge-on-card are rAF/Three-gated → flagged for manual; `selectClaim` + `_isNew` mirror the verified RFP pattern). **Part B** — footer Publish button **disabled + `not-allowed` + tooltip** on the published PRM Assembly Claim, **enabled** on the unpublished PCB Stackup; V22ActionBar **parity confirmed** (the card's ⊕ button is disabled + `not-allowed` + parity tooltip); the `_publishedToDirectory` flag is correct across all 8 of Alice's Claims (3 seeded-public = true, 5 umbrella-seed = false). **Part C** — Parse modal title "Parse Asset" + "Parse Asset →" button + footer "Parse Asset"; the **missing-PDF Asset (RF Filter RFF-900 → `/microco-rffilter.pdf`) shows "No preview available"** (no app-in-iframe), the **real-PDF Asset (PRM Datasheet → `/seed-pdfs/microco-prm-datasheet.pdf`) renders the iframe** (no regression). Revoke→re-enable (B), Selective/Proof-Only orchestration parity (A), defensive dispatch gate (B), and RFP-routing regression (A leaves `routeToRfp` untouched) are code-verified. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.3.
- 2026-05-22: **Phase 18.2.2 — Retire `PublishToDirectoryModal`, reuse `CombinedResponseModal`.** The bespoke publish modal shipped in 18.2 is **deleted** (`src/components/modals/PublishToDirectoryModal.jsx`); the Publish-to-Directory flow now runs through `CombinedResponseModal` via a new `directoryPublishMode` prop. Mental model: publishing is "disclose to the Radiant Network as a non-human auto-accepting grantee" — structurally identical to the cold-path Disclosure Response minus the EA half — so it reuses the same modal and primitives, parity-by-construction with the responder flow. **11 surgical branches in `CombinedResponseModal.jsx`:** new prop + derived `isDirectoryPublish`; `totalSteps` short-circuits to `3`; `handleSubmit` fires DA-only `onAccept({ type, scope, daTerms: { expires } })`; banner shows "Publishing to / Radiant Network" (no Requester/Message rows); header titles/subtitles branch ("Publish to the Public Directory" / "Review your Public Directory Disclosure"); Step 1 grid drops the Decline card (4 → 3 cols) + relabels "Choose disclosure type"; Step 2 scope descriptions swap counterparty-name copy for "the Public Directory" / "Actors browsing the Public Directory"; Step 3 EA-Terms gated `&& !isDirectoryPublish`; Step 4 review gate moves `step === 4` → `step === totalSteps` + hides the EA-Expires + Acknowledgments rows; Accept button reads "Publish to Directory". **V2App:** removed the `PublishToDirectoryModal` import + mount; new mount uses `CombinedResponseModal directoryPublishMode={true}` with a synthesized `request` (active actor `ownerParty`, `'Radiant Network'` `requesterParty`, `proposedEaTerms: null`) + the same `referencedAssets`/`parseResults`/`poesForClaim` contract as the cold-path mount; `handlePublishToDirectorySubmit` payload field renamed `payload.expiryIso` → `payload.daTerms?.expires`. Stale comment refs in V2App + V22NodeDetailPanel repointed. **Behavior delta:** the Full Asset picker now defaults to **zero-selected** (the CombinedResponseModal picker convention per CLAUDE.md), vs the retired modal's all-checked default — the user explicitly opts in. **Runtime-verified** (Alice/MicroCo, PRM Assembly Claim; build clean; zero console errors): the publish modal opens in directoryPublishMode — title "Publish to the Public Directory", "Step 1 of 3", banner "Publishing to / Radiant Network" + "Claim", 3 cards (Full/Selective/Proof-Only, **no Decline**); Step 2 Full shows the DA-expiry picker + Asset list with publish-specific copy ("the Public Directory will surface" / "accessible from the Public Directory"); Step 3 Review shows DISCLOSURE TYPE / ASSETS IN SCOPE / DISCLOSURE AGREEMENT EXPIRES — **no EA-Expires row, no Acknowledgments row** — and a "Publish to Directory" button; **the Full publish is confirmed end-to-end** (Submit → modal closes → a "Full Disclosure · with Radiant Network" row appears in the Claim's Agreements section); the **Proof-Only Step 2** PoE picker renders both third-party PoEs ("by GovCo · wraps 1 · 8 SAT · 1 UNSAT", "by AuditCo · wraps 1 · 2 SAT · 1 UNSAT", neither owned by Alice — confirms the 18.2.1.1 Claim-membership filter carries over) with publish copy ("Actors browsing the Public Directory…"). **Cold-path regression** (4 cards / 4 steps / EA-Expires + Acknowledgments rows) + EA-only warm path + Decline path are preserved **by construction** — every new branch is gated behind `isDirectoryPublish` (false for those flows) and the build is clean — but couldn't be live-confirmed in-session (no seeded `v22-request` notification + canvas card-selection harness limits); flagged for a manual pass (QA #13–15). `grep -r PublishToDirectoryModal src/` returns zero. - 2026-05-22: **Phase 18.2.1.1 — V2App PoE picker filter hotfix.** Single-symbol fix at two parallel V2App modal-mount sites. Both the `CombinedResponseModal` Proof-Only step (`poesForClaim`) and the `AmendDisclosureModal` Proof-Only scope picker (`candidatePoEs`) pre-filtered PoEs with `poe.claimId === <claim> && poe.owner === da.grantor.party`. The owner clause was wrong: per Phase 13 / spec §10.4, a proof-only Claim DA carries `subject.kind === 'claim'` + `scope.poeIds`, and the grantor's right to grant proof-only disclosure derives from their ownership of the **Claim**, not the PoE — so any PoE visible to the grantor on that Claim (including third-party-authored PoEs that reached the claim owner via the original evaluator → claim-owner proof-of-evaluation DA) is a valid scope candidate. The clause excluded every third-party-authored PoE (the common case — surfaced by 18.2.1 QA: Carol's PoE on Alice's EMI Shield Claim showed the empty state when Bob requested + Alice responded with Proof-Only) and only matched self-evaluation PoEs. Fixed by dropping the clause at both sites (filter by Claim membership only) — matching the seed precedent (Phase 17.5.0.4) + Phase 18.2's correctly-wired `PublishToDirectoryModal` filter. The new filter is a strict superset of the old, so self-evaluation PoEs (`owner === grantor`) still match. No view-builder, modal, or data-model changes (the picker rows already show `by {owner}` evaluator attribution). **Verification:** build clean; the fix is a deterministic filter-predicate change (drop one `&&` clause at each site) — code- + build-verified + data-probe confirmed (the seeded `poeBobPrm`/`poeCarolPrm` both carry `claimId === cPrm.id` with owners GovCo/AuditCo ≠ MicroCo, so the old filter excluded both and the new filter includes both for Alice as grantor). Live confirmation of the response/amend modal is blocked in-session by the documented no-seeded-`v22-request` + canvas-card-selection harness limits; flagged for a manual pass. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.1.1.
- 2026-05-22: **Phase 18.2.1 — CombinedResponseModal hotfix (Step 1 button gating + Proof-Only copy parity).** Two surgical fixes to `CombinedResponseModal.jsx` surfaced during Phase 18.2 QA; both predate 18.2 (Phase 11/13-era modal). **Bug 1 (P0 crash):** the cold-path `totalSteps` formula `(isDecline ? 2 : action ? 4 : 1)` collapsed to `1` at Step 1 before a type was picked, so `step === totalSteps` was true at Step 1 → the ungated final-step "Accept" button rendered instead of the disabled "Continue"; clicking it fired `handleSubmit()` with `action === null`, propagating null `type` + `scope` into V2App's accept handler → crash. Fixed by hardcoding the non-decline cold path to `4` (`(isDecline ? 2 : 4)`) — StepDots reads "Step 1 of 4" from open, Continue renders gated by the pre-existing `canAdvanceFromStep1 = action != null`, Accept only renders at `step === 4`. **Bug 2 (copy regression):** swept the Step 2 Proof-Only branch's stale "Evaluation Results" copy → "Proofs of Evaluation" (the picker was already migrated to PoEs in Phase 13 #168): the FieldLabel, the description, the empty-state copy + styling (now neutral `var(--bg-card)`/`var(--border)`/`var(--text-dim)`, byte-matched to the Selective empty state; copy mirrors Selective's "No X available. Y Disclosure requires at least one Z…"), and the Step 4 review row label ("Proofs of Evaluation shared"). No data-model, handler, or prop changes. **Verification:** build clean; both fixes are deterministic render-logic (Bug 1, traced exactly) + copy/style swaps (Bug 2), code- + build-verified. Live confirmation of the cold-path response modal is blocked in-session by the absence of any seeded `v22-request` notification (generating one needs an undisclosed-Claim PIN + a multi-role request→respond flow) — flagged for a manual pass per the documented harness limitation. Console shows only the pre-existing benign `NaN`-CSS-`left` edge-menu transient (unrelated). Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.1.
- 2026-05-22: **Phase 18.2 — Publish Claims to the Public Directory (foundation).** Restores the V2.0/V2.1 publish-to-Radiant-Network mechanic lost in the V2.2 Directory rebuild. New two-step `PublishToDirectoryModal` (Form → Review, cloned from `RfpCreationModal`): Full/Selective/Proof-Only disclosure-type picker (Proof-Only disabled with `cursor: not-allowed` + tooltip when the Claim has no PoEs) + a type-branched scope picker (Full → Asset checkboxes; Selective → expandable Asset rows with parsed-field checkboxes, composite ids `${parseResultId}::${fieldId}`; Proof-Only → PoE checkboxes) + an `ExpiryPicker` (default "Never expires"). Owner-only entry points: V22ClaimPanel footer ("Publish to Directory", icon `⊕`, after Amend Claim + Self-Evaluate) + V22ActionBar CLAIM branch (`onV22CardAction('publishToDirectory')`). On Submit, `handlePublishToDirectorySubmit` mints a DA via `makeDisclosureAgreement` (grantee = Radiant Network, picked `type`, discriminated `scope`, `status: 'active'`) and pushes it into `v22Provisionals.disclosureAgreements`. **No new merge helper or builder signature change** — `mergeProvisionals` + the existing `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` public-DA detection surface the Claim as a dot in the owner's cluster on next render. Radiant Network is an auto-accepting grantee → no provisional/accept handshake. **Runtime-verified** (Alice, MicroCo): Voltage Regulator IC Claim panel shows the `⊕ Publish to Directory` footer button; the modal opens with all three disclosure-type radios + Step 2 review (FOR CLAIM / GRANTED TO Radiant Network + globe / DISCLOSURE TYPE / SCOPE / EXPIRES); the **Full publish flow is confirmed end-to-end** — Publish closes the modal and a new "Full Disclosure · with Radiant Network · Never expires" row (with AMEND/REVOKE) appears in the Claim's Agreements section, no console errors. Proof-Only **disabled gating** confirmed on a no-PoE Claim (`cursor: not-allowed` + the exact tooltip "This Claim has no Proofs of Evaluation yet…", read from the React fiber). The Selective field-picker UI + Proof-Only-enabled PoE list + the Directory dot rendering are **code/data-verified** (deterministic from the seed — `poeBobPrm`/`poeCarolPrm.claimId === cPrm.id`; confirmed `${pr.id}::${f.id}` field format; the unchanged Directory builder) but were **not live-confirmed in-session** — canvas card-selection after a role-switch + FIT couldn't reliably place Alice's PRM-Assembly card on-screen, and Directory dots are canvas-raycaster-gated (documented V2Canvas harness limitations). Build clean. Post-creation orchestration (auto-route + variant load animation + auto-select + NEW badge) lands in **Phase 18.2.1**. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.2.
- 2026-05-22: **Phase 18.1 — Placeholder RELEASE action on Internal Disclosure Agreement rows.** Every Internal DA row (`isInternal && !isRevoked`) in the shared `<AgreementsSection>` now renders a muted, disabled `<ActionLabel label="Release">` in the AMEND slot of `DisclosureAgreementRow` — propagating across Actor / Asset / Claim / Eval Result / Parse Result Detail Panels via the single shared component (no prop plumbing). The tooltip noun resolves from the DA's own `subject.kind` through a new module-scoped `DA_SUBJECT_KIND_LABEL` map (asset→Asset, claim→Claim, evalResult→Evaluation Result, parseResult→Parse Result, poe→Proof of Evaluation; `'artifact'` fallback) — not the panel node type — so it accurately describes what releasing *that* agreement would affect ("Releasing your disclosure agreement to this [noun] will remove it from your network. Disabled-only surface — no `onClick`, no handler, no data-model changes; previews the future "release this artifact from my network" mechanism. **Runtime-verified** (Bob's Avionics Module Asset panel): 3 Internal DA rows render RELEASE (muted `var(--text-dim)` `rgb(122,131,153)`, cursor default); subject-aware tooltips confirmed via the React fiber — Row 1 (asset subject) "...to this Asset...", Rows 2-3 (evalResult subjects) "...to this Evaluation Result..." (proving the noun is subject-driven, not panel-driven, since all three are on an Asset panel); clicking RELEASE is a no-op (panel unchanged, no console errors); the 2 cross-party AMEND + 18 REVOKE rows are unchanged. Build clean. Footer rolls forward to v0.18.1.
- 2026-05-21: **Phase 18.0.1 — Hotfix: sticky edge tooltip on Parent → Directory transition (closes #203).** Demo-blocking bug Andrew hit recording the RFP walkthrough — the parent-canvas "SELECT EDGE TO VIEW" hover tooltip persisted over the Directory layer after transitioning. **Root cause:** V2Canvas's `onMouseMove`-bound `handleEdgeHover` had no Directory-open guard (only the `transitioningRef` dive/surface check). 17.4.3's `clearEdgeUiState` cleared all three edge-tooltip state layers correctly + synchronously, but any mousemove during/after the ~600ms Directory wipe re-raycasted a parent edge → re-fired `onEdgeHover` → re-set V2App's `edgeHover` → the `EdgeHoverMenu` re-rendered (z-index 6000, portaled to document.body) over the Directory. **Fix:** (1) new `directoryOpen` prop on V2Canvas; early-return guards in `handleEdgeHover` + `handleBackgroundClick`; a `useEffect` that clears V2Canvas's internal hover state + cancels the 150ms debounce on the `directoryOpen` false→true flip. (2) V2App passes `directoryOpen={v22DirectoryOpen}`; defensive `!v22DirectoryOpen` gate on both `EdgeHoverMenu` mounts; `setEdgeMenuPanning(false)` added to `clearEdgeUiState` for completeness (the prompt's `setEdgeMenuProjected` doesn't exist — `edgeMenuPanning` is the real fade/world-tracking flag). All Directory-open paths already call `clearEdgeUiState`; the V2Canvas guard makes the system robust to a missed clear. **Runtime status:** build clean; no console errors; directory open/close cycle verified clean; ~900 parent-canvas mousemoves while Directory open produced no tooltip ghost + no errors (exercises the `directoryOpen` guard). The hover-tooltip repro itself is canvas-raycaster-gated (DOM-dispatched mousemoves don't trigger the Three.js edge raycaster) → verified via the guard path + code review. **#203 marked ✅ Complete and moved to the Completed section.** Footer rolls forward to v0.18.0.1.

- 2026-05-21: **Phase 17.5.2.2 — RFP arc post-closure cleanup.** Four manual-QA items after the arc closed at 17.5.2.1. (1) **Request Agreement regression on user-created RFPs:** `handleRequestAgreement` resolved the RFP via bare `buildV22SharedArtifacts()` (no session-state overlays), so it bailed at `if (!rfp) return` for user-created RFPs — same bug shape as 17.5.1.3's `handleCreateSolicitation`. V2App computes `!!(v22DirectorySelectedClaim || v22DirectorySelectedRfp)`. **Runtime status:** build clean; no console errors. Code-verified across all four parts; the pan/zoom-to-60% pacing + notification round-trips are rAF-driven and the headless preview throttles rAF (~1fps), so the in-browser animation pacing is **flagged for manual verification** (documented Three.js/preview limitation) — the logic mirrors the verified notification-routing path. **The RFP arc is fully closed — no open follow-ups.** Queue opens to deferred items: Directory Layer enhancements, AI Shopper, Cascading Disclosures (#26), remaining Claim Detail Panel content (#58/#104/#116/#161/#180), Netgraph cleanup (#4/#130), seed-resilience carry-overs #204/#205. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2.2.
- 2026-05-21: **Phase 17.5.2.1 — RFP arc closing polish (regression fix + transition smoothing + visual cleanup).** Six bundled items. (1) **Tab-crash regression fixed** on the 17.5.1.5 Anchored RFPs row click. Root cause: `routeToRfp`'s rAF select-retry chain was never cancelled when re-invoked, and 17.5.2 raised its cap 60 → 240 frames (~4s @ 60fps) — so repeated RFP navigation (Anchored-RFPs click → For-Asset round trip → click again) stacked multiple concurrent 4-second chains, each filtering the ~23k-entry `allDots` every frame and firing `setPinned`/pan-zoom on resolution, saturating the main thread → tab freeze. Fix: hold the in-flight chain's rAF id in `routeRfpSelectRafRef`, cancel it at the start of `routeToRfp` (and zero it on terminate), and cancel it in `routeToAsset` too — only one chain is ever live. (2) **Smooth Directory → Parent transition:** a `phase === 'out'` effect in DirectoryLayer eases the camera zoom outward (current → `DIRECTORY_EXIT_ZOOM` ≈ 0.6, `Math.min` for outward-only) over the 900ms reverse wipe, parallel to the Parent pre-positioning `routeToAsset` already fires. Applies to every close path (globe button, exit chip, double-click, For-Asset). (3) **Anchored RFPs row restructure:** icons dropped from CLOSE/REOPEN/REMOVE (text-only); REOPEN+REMOVE stack vertically when both render (closed RFP), matching the AMEND/REVOKE EA row column (flex column, flex-end, gap 4, minWidth 52); REMOVE keeps `danger` red. (4+5) **ASSET / ACTOR header badges** added to their Detail Panel headers via `PanelHeader typeLabel`, matching the existing CLAIM/EVAL RESULT/PARSE RESULT/PoE/BADGE TEMPLATE convention (reverses the 17.3 #202 removal for these two). (6) **Changelog modal header copy:** "Prototype Changelog" → "Changelog", "Radiant V2 — PCN Prototyping" → "Radiant v2.2". **Runtime status:** build clean; no console errors. Parts 1, 3, 4, 5, 6 **confirmed in dev preview** (anchored-RFP click routes cleanly with no freeze + clean round-trip; open-RFP CLOSE text-only; closed-RFP REOPEN/REMOVE stacked + red REMOVE; ASSET + ACTOR badges render; Changelog header "Changelog"/"Radiant v2.2"). Part 2's camera zoom-out (rAF-driven) is **flagged for manual verification** — the headless preview throttles rAF (~1fps) so animation pacing can't be confirmed in-session; the parent pre-positioning + close path are confirmed and the zoom-out is structurally wired into the verified close `phase` effect (documented Three.js/preview limitation). **The RFP creation arc is now fully CLOSED** (#192 Item J done across 17.5 → 17.5.0.x → 17.5.1 → 17.5.1.x → 17.5.2 → 17.5.2.1). Queue opens to deferred items: Directory Layer enhancements, AI Shopper, Cascading Disclosures (#26), remaining Claim Detail Panel content (#58/#104/#116/#161/#180), Netgraph cleanup (#4/#130), seed-resilience carry-overs #204/#205. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2.1.
- 2026-05-21: **Phase 17.5.2 — Post-creation orchestration + bidirectional Parent↔Directory navigation (closes the RFP creation arc).** Three navigation/transition pieces. (A) **Post-creation orchestration:** `handleRfpCreationSubmit` now stamps `v22RecentlyCreatedRfpId` (drives a NEW badge on the synthetic RFP card via a `recentlyCreatedRfpId` prop → `_isNew` → AssetNode) and fires `routeToRfp(newRfp.id, { rfp: newRfp })` — switches Parent → Directory (forward wipe), pans/zooms to the new marker at full-card LOD, auto-opens its Detail Panel. `routeToRfp` already zoomed to LOD_THRESHOLD, so no zoom-level plumbing was needed; an `opts.rfp` fallback covers the stale-`v22CreatedRfps`-at-submit-time case. A clear-on-deselect effect drops `_isNew` when the user navigates away. (B) **RFP → Asset nav:** the RfpDetailPanel "For Asset" row is now clickable (hover tint + pointer + chevron) → new `routeToAsset(assetId)` helper closes the Directory + Detail Panels (reverse wipe) and selects/pan-zooms to the Asset on Parent (inverse of 17.5.1.5's click-to-open-RFP). (C) **Double-click escape:** DirectoryLayer's canvas gains an `onDoubleClick` that fires the reverse wipe (`onClose`) on empty-space dblclicks, gated on `phase === 'in'` + `e.target === renderer.domElement` + raycast-miss so markers/labels/cards/chrome don't trigger it. Two robustness follow-ups landed during verification: the entry cinematic zoom-out (17.3.2) is skipped when routing to a just-created RFP (gated on a `recentlyCreatedRfpIdRef`) and `animatedPanToWithZoom` cancels any in-flight cinematic lerp, plus `routeToRfp`'s rAF select-retry cap was raised 60 → 240. **#192 Item J fully closed** (the RFP creation flow + the whole creation arc 17.5 → 17.5.0.x → 17.5.1 → 17.5.1.x → 17.5.2 are done). Build clean. **Runtime status:** auto-route Parent → Directory + auto-open Detail Panel **confirmed**; double-click-empty-space → Parent **confirmed** (handler fires the reverse wipe for the Directory-canvas target, ignores parent-canvas/marker/label targets). The auto-zoom to full-card LOD + NEW badge + For-Asset click-landing were **NOT visually confirmed in-preview** (in repeated runs `selectRfp` didn't pin/zoom the freshly-created marker — Directory stayed at galactic zoom; the preview was also destabilized by a 2px viewport + repeated reloads) — flagged for manual verification; the code mirrors the verified 17.2.0.2 notification-routing + `handleViewEa` patterns. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.2. **Next: 17.5.2.1** (visual polish — action label restructure + missing ASSET/ACTOR badges); if the post-creation zoom/badge doesn't land in manual review, a follow-up to make `selectRfp`-on-create deterministic (a DirectoryLayer-internal select-on-layout-ready effect) is the fix.

Each row: line 1 = RFP name + status pill (green OPEN / muted CLOSED), line 2 = "Posted {date} · N requirements"; the row body click-navigates to the RFP's Detail Panel on the Directory layer via a new reusable `routeToRfp(rfpId)` helper (extracted from the `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` notification routing — closes the Asset panel, opens Directory, selectRfp with rAF retry). Non-owner rows show identity + navigation only. The handlers reuse `handleCloseRfp`/`handleReopenRfp`/`handleRemoveRfp` (17.5.1.4), so Close from a row fires solicitor notifications + Remove silently cascades, and status flips in-place on the row (the panel recomputes `anchoredRfpsForNode` from the merge chain each render). This is the parent-canvas surface for RFPs (which are otherwise Directory-layer-only). Build clean. Affected leaks now fixed (probe-verified): `cChipcoFlashMem` + `cChipcoAdcDac` + `cMicroThermalPad` on Bob's canvas; `cChipcoAdcDac` on Alice's canvas; `bAvionics` + `cAuditWorkspace` on Dave's canvas. EA-paired pull-ins intact (Bob ← cPrm/cVreg; Carol ← cPrm/cEmi; Alice ← bAvionics/cAuditWorkspace). (2) 17 new stub Assets added; 12 Dave catalog Claims + 5 Alice umbrella-seed Claims now reference proper Asset anchors (existing `claimRefEdges` loop emits the `da-ref-` DAs; no new duplicate-id collisions). (3) LibraryModal Published Requirements empty state now shows the wire-globe icon matching the other tabs. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.4. Next phase: 17.5.1 — RfpCreationModal scaffolding.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.5.0.3 — Universal neutral footer buttons.** Completes the "all buttons neutral" policy from Phase 17.5.0.1 by dropping `accent` from every remaining FooterButton call site across Actor / Claim / Eval Result / PoE / Badge Template panels. 11 call sites touched (12 `accent` keywords — Create PoE has two branches). `danger` and `amber` variants preserved (Cancel Transfer, Cancel Request, Respond to Request). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.3.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.5.0.2 — Cross-panel footer icon propagation + visible-but-disabled audit.** Completes the Detail Panel footer styling work begun in 17.5 / 17.5.0.1 — every `FooterButton` call site in `V22NodeDetailPanel.jsx` (Actor / Claim / Eval Result / PoE / Badge Template) now uses the icon-with-hover-expand-label pattern. Icons reuse V22ActionBar glyphs everywhere they overlap; three footer-only actions get new picks (Dismiss `⊠`, Respond to Request `↪`, Create new version `＋`). Create PoE on the Eval Result panel now renders visible-but-disabled when the chain is PoE-terminated, mirroring Re-Run's existing pattern (also required dropping the `!node._alreadyWrapped` gate on V2App's `onCreatePoE` prop so the handler reaches the panel). **Flagged out of scope** per the stop-and-flag rule: `RfpDetailPanel.jsx` (uses a local `ActionButton`) and the DA / EA agreement panels (own button components) are NOT `FooterButton` consumers — their footer-icon migration is deferred to a follow-up rather than picking icons unilaterally. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.2. Next phase: 17.5.1 — RfpCreationModal scaffolding.
