Scoped to the icon-mode branch (the Asset footer); the legacy label-only branch on other panels is untouched. Cross-panel propagation of the 17.5 icon-with-hover-expand pattern is the next polish or build phase per the active queue. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.0.1.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.5 — RFP creation entry-point foundation.** First phase of the 17.5.x arc (user-facing RFP creation flow — third leg of the Directory layer triad: browse + connect + create). Asset Detail Panel footer migrated from label-only to icon-with-hover-expand-label pattern, closing the Phase 10.2 "intentionally crowded for now" flag and fixing the existing Transfer cut-off. The `FooterButton` `icon` prop gates the new behavior — call sites that pass no icon keep the legacy label-only button, so the ~18 other footers (Claim / Eval Result / dismiss / etc.) are unaffected. New 6th action "Create RFP" added on both the V22ActionBar ASSET branch and the Asset Detail Panel footer; both dispatch `createRfp` via the existing `onV22CardAction` machinery. `handleCreateRfp` in V2App is a placeholder logger in 17.5 — the RfpCreationModal lands in 17.5.1. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.5.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.6 — Backlog hygiene.** Backlog-only — filed #204 (extract a `makeOwnershipDAsForActor` helper so per-actor internal-DA seeding can't repeat the 17.4.5 over-iteration bug) and #205 (drop the duplicate actor-specific `da-ref` / `da-parse` seed blocks in favor of the generic loops). Both are seed-resilience carry-overs from the 17.4.5 post-mortem. No code or version changes. Round 18 wraps here; Round 19 picks up at Phase 17.5 (RFP creation flow).

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.5 — Internal "ownership" DA leak fix on Directory Claim panel.** When Alice (MicroCo) viewed a ChipCo Claim's Detail Panel on the Directory layer, the Agreements section showed ChipCo's internal ownership DA ("Full Disclosure — Internal — Never expires") next to her legitimate umbrella DA. **Diagnosis (not the brief's hypothesized panel/filter scenarios):** a seed-data bug. The panel renders strictly from `disclosureAgreementsForNode` (no fallback), and the V2App Directory mount filter is correct. The real cause: `v2_2Data.js`'s `aliceOwnClaims = claims.map(...)` loop iterated the *full* primary-Claim array (Alice's + Dave/ChipCo's + the Phase 17.4 MicroCo Claims) while hardcoding `owner: alice.party`, minting spurious MicroCo→MicroCo internal ownership DAs over every ChipCo Claim — with `da-own-${c.id}` ids that collide with `daveOwnClaims`'. The MicroCo/MicroCo duplicate passes the party-presence filter because Alice IS both parties of the internal DA, so it surfaced. Note the brief's proposed rule ("internal DA where active actor is *neither* party") wouldn't catch this — Alice is a party to the bogus DA. **Fix:** restrict `aliceOwnClaims` to `claims.filter((c) => c.owner === alice.party)`. Owner-side preserved (Dave still sees his ChipCo internal DA on his own Claims). Probe-verified across all four roles + parent-canvas view builds. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.5.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.4 — Revoke wiring on Directory Claim panel + #203 backlog entry.** Two small follow-ups closing out the umbrella-disclosure arc. **Revoke wiring**: the Directory V22ClaimPanel mount (`v22DirectorySelectedClaim`) rendered the umbrella DA row with a Revoke button that did nothing — the mount was missing the `onRevokeDa` / `onRevokeEa` props the parent-canvas mount passes. Wired both through to the existing `handleOpenRevocationConfirm(agreement, type)`, which already handles grantee-side revocation (it branches on `agreement.grantor.party === activeRole.party`). No new handler logic needed; the reactive flow (`setV22Provisionals` → `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` filtering `!d._revokedMeta`) reshapes the perimeter and drops umbrella-only Claims from the cluster automatically. Stop-and-surface check passed: `V22NodeDetailPanel` gates the Revoke button on agreement state (`showRevoke = (isGrantor || isGrantee) && …`), with only the `onClick` gated on the prop — so wiring the prop activates the button. **#203 filed**: rare edge-tooltip stickiness across layer transition (Round 19 residual) — `clearEdgeUiState` closes the common paths but a stuck tooltip was reproduced once during QA; not blocking. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.4.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.3 — Polish wrap fix: Minkowski perimeter + legend z + edge tooltip cleanup.** Two 17.4.2 items still misbehaving + a long-standing ghost-tooltip bug. **Minkowski perimeter**: `umbrellaOutlinePath` 3+ branch now uses `collinearStadium` (Minkowski-with-disk capsule) unconditionally — the 17.4.2 `isCollinear` epsilon was too tight, so near-collinear subsets (Bob's 7 ChipCo umbrella dots in a vertical strip) still rendered as a line via `offsetPolygonOutward` on a thin hull. `isCollinear` + `offsetPolygonOutward` retained but marked unused. **Legend z**: `DirectoryLegendBar` `zIndex 50 → 400` above the active cluster label (300). **Edge tooltip cleanup**: new `clearEdgeUiState()` helper (clearHoverState + setEdgeHover + setEdgeMenu) called from all three Directory-open paths + the role-switch handler — V2App's `edgeHover`/`edgeMenu` (pinned "SELECT EDGE TO VIEW" box) were never cleared on layer/role change, ghosting over the Directory + reappearing on role switch-back. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.3.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.2 — Polish wrap fix: collinear perimeter + legend positioning + distance-based active-cluster buffer.** Three 17.4.1 items that visual QA showed didn't land. **Collinear perimeter**: a straight-grid-row umbrella subset made `convexHull` degenerate + `offsetPolygonOutward` rendered it as a line; new `isCollinear` test routes the degenerate case to a new `collinearStadium` (Minkowski-sum-with-disk capsule). 2D subsets unchanged. **Legend positioning**: `DirectoryLegendBar` `bottom: 12 → 40` to clear the global app footer that was covering it. **Distance-based active-cluster buffer**: removed the 17.4.1 area-based `4 × DOT_GRID²` buffer (only ~1 visible dot of gap; Lloyd's redistributes non-isotropically); replaced by a post-Lloyd's seed correction pushing neighbour seeds out to a ≥ 8 DOT_GRID edge-to-edge gap, then recompute the final Voronoi. Seeds clamp to canvas + warn on clamp-short. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.2.

- 2026-05-20: **Phase 17.4.1 — Polish wrap: perimeter shape fix + Directory legend + active-cluster buffer + active label z.** Four QA-driven polish items on the umbrella DA arc. **Perimeter inflation halved** (`DOT_GRID` → `DOT_GRID / 2`) across all three `umbrellaOutlinePath` branches so corners sit at the midpoint between umbrella dots and adjacent non-umbrella dots (was over-extending a full cell). **New DirectoryLegendBar** (bottom-left, mirrors V2Canvas LegendBar): Full/Selective/Proof-Only disclosure dots (indigo/amber/green) + RFP hollow square; inline hover tooltips. **4-dot buffer** added to the active actor's own cluster in the Lloyd's target-area computation so neighbouring mock clusters stop crowding it. **Active actor label z-index** bumped to a dedicated `Z_ACTIVE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 300` above the other cluster labels. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4.1.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.4 — Umbrella DA arc: perimeter fix + seed expansion + Directory panel/card parity + perimeter hover.** Andrew's Option #3 (grouping perimeter, no edges) ratified. **Perimeter rendering fixed**: `packClusterDense` was placing the umbrella subset on the innermost cells (ringing the central label hole), so the convex-hull perimeter enclosed the hole + actor pillbox. The packer now relocates the umbrella subset into a coherent edge-blob (anchored at the inner cell furthest from center) so the hull wraps just the umbrella dots. **Seed expansion**: 14 new umbrella DAs (Alice→Bob 5, Alice→Carol 3, Dave→Alice 3, Dave→Carol 3) + 5 new non-public MicroCo Claims (MicroCo's existing 3 are all public; public takes precedence over umbrella). Per-role demo: Bob ChipCo(7)+MicroCo(5), Alice ChipCo(3), Carol MicroCo(3)+ChipCo(3), Dave none. **Directory V22ClaimPanel parity**: DA/EA Agreements section + warm-path Request EA footer button now threaded to the Directory mount (cold-path + View-EA from 17.3 preserved; mutation handlers omitted as grantor-side). **Card action bar warm-path parity**: new `_directoryWarmPathRequestCandidate` marker, mutually exclusive with cold-path + View-EA. **Perimeter hover + tooltip**: point-in-polygon hit-test (independent of dot raycast); hover brightens border + fill; HTML tooltip with disclosure-explainer + "From {grantor}" sub-line. **Umbrella DA arc complete** pending the deferred parent-layer EA-required-message bug (Andrew can't recall the symptom — deferred unless it resurfaces). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.4. Reference #192 (RFP arc) is unrelated; this closes the umbrella-DA visualisation work.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.3.2 — Fan-out improvements + leak investigation + RFP owner card action bar.** Five items batched before Phase 17.4 opens. **Fan-out jitter** bumped 250 → 600 ms; **wave speed** bumped 7000 → 9500 wu/sec — wavefront travels faster and jitter is now a larger fraction of the timeline for a more pronounced jagged organic edge. **CSS wipe duration** 550 → 900 ms so wipe + fan-out feel like one continuous transition. **Cinematic zoom-out**: camera starts at `INITIAL_ZOOM × 1.3` (30% tighter) and eases out to `INITIAL_ZOOM` over 1500 ms via `easeOut(t) = 1 - (1-t)^3`. **Safari crash investigation outcome**: root cause WebGL context exhaustion — surgical fix applied via `WEBGL_lose_context.loseContext()` in scene cleanup before `renderer.dispose()`. **RFP owner Close/Reopen action bar** on full-size card (parity with panel footer); DirectoryLayer stamps `_directoryCloseCandidate` / `_directoryReopenCandidate` markers, AssetNode renders padlock-icon buttons, V2App routes through the same `setV22ClosedRfpIds` updates the panel uses. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.2.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.3.1 — Polish wrap: RFP card action bar + RfpDetailPanel cleanup + Requirements rows + fan-out animation tweaks.** Four items batched before Phase 17.4 (umbrella DA edges) opens. **RFP full-size card action bar** parallel to the Claim card pattern shipped in 17.3 — DirectoryLayer stamps `_directorySolicitCandidate` on synthetic RFP nodes when the active actor is a non-owner of an open RFP with no existing solicitation; AssetNode's RFP early-return renders an inline action bar with a paper-airplane SVG "Solicit with my Claim" button on hover/select; click dispatches `solicitWithClaim` and V2App routes to the same SolicitationCreateModal the panel footer opens. **RfpDetailPanel categorical pill cleanup** extends #202 from 17.3: removed the "ACTOR" pill from the "POSTED BY" row and the "ASSET" pill from the "FOR ASSET" row. Section headers + "YOU" indicator preserved. **#202 is now fully closed across all Detail Panel surfaces.** **Requirements section overhaul**: section title renamed "Required Standards" → "Requirements"; layout converted from horizontal chips to vertical full-width clickable rows with a small version badge pill on the right; click opens a new RequirementsSetDetailModal. Missing-RS rows render dashed-border muted "Standard not found ({rsId})" non-clickable. **New RequirementsSetDetailModal** (`src/components/modals/RequirementsSetDetailModal.jsx`) — read-only single-RS view: header (name + version badge), sub-header (Published by + globe icon), scrollable body listing each requirement as id-pill + label + description + optional Criterion. ESC + backdrop close. **Fan-out loading animation tweaks**: `directoryLoadAnimation.js` waveSpeed 4500 → 7000 wu/sec; dotFadeMs 200 → 90 ms; new `jitterMs` (250 ms) seeded random per-instance start offset gives a jagged organic wavefront; eased via `easeOut(t)` instead of linear; RFP markers participate via new `setRfpAppear` callback. **DirectoryLayer per-instance scale-driven appearance** replaces the 16.2.3 color-multiplier-from-black pattern — `dotOpacitiesRef` reinterpreted from color multiplier to matrix scale factor (0 = invisible, 1 = full size); dots render at final color from the moment they appear; the wave grows each dot from a point to its full size. New `flushDotMatrices` + `flushRfpMatrices` callbacks integrated into the existing animate-loop dirty-flag pattern. `flushDotColors` simplified. RFP hit-test mesh keeps full scale (clickable mid-wave). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.1.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.3 — Claim Detail Panel EA content on Directory + card action bar + #202 ACTOR/ASSET label cleanup.** Opens the action surface for Directory-layer Claim discovery against polish-backlog **#202** (closed) plus partial coverage of Claim Detail Panel content items (#58, #104, #116, #161, #180 — remaining scope continues in 17.3.x or later detail-panel polish phases). V22NodeDetailPanel Claim view gains an EA-status section near the top of the body — "An Evaluation Agreement is required to evaluate this Claim." (no-EA cold-path, amber callout) or "An Evaluation Agreement is in place with {owner}." (EA-exists, indigo callout). Footer extensions match: "Request Evaluation Agreement" (cold-path, opens AssetPickerModal) or "View Evaluation Agreement" (closes Directory + pans parent canvas to Claim node + selects EA edge + opens EA Detail Panel via existing setOpenAgreement mechanism). Owner viewing own Claim gets no EA-status section. Pre-existing Phase 11C warm-path preserved unchanged. AssetNode V22ActionBar Claim branch surfaces parallel CTAs on Directory full-size Claim cards (Request EA ▷ / View EA ◉) via two new synthetic-node stamps (`_directoryRequestEaCandidate` / `_directoryExistingEa`). DirectoryLayer's card-overlay render path resolves the EA via new shared predicate `getActiveEaForClaimAndRequester` exported from v2_2Data.js. AssetPickerModal re-mounted in V2App for the cold-path Directory CTA, with generalized prop contract (targetClaim + context = directory-claim | rfp). Three new V2App handlers: handleRequestEaForClaim, handleAssetPickedForClaim, handleViewEa. **#202 closed** by removing ACTOR + ASSET typeLabel pills from V22ActorPanel + V22AssetPanel headers (PanelHeader's typeLabel prop is now optional; other panel types keep their type pill since those are abstract artifact concepts). Parent-layer EA-required-message bug still deferred to Phase 17.4+ alongside umbrella DA edge work. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.3.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.2.1.2 — Hotfix: CombinedRequestModal RS pre-selection + Detail Panel label backlog entry.** Closes the last QA item against the 17.2 arc. Root cause: `handleRequestAgreement` only stored the first id of `rfp.requirementsSetIds` (`suggestedRequirementsSetId` singular) and the mount block wrapped it back into `[id]`, so multi-RS RFPs (like Bob's Sentinel-4 referencing `MIL-PRF-55681 Compliance v2` + `System Integration Requirements v1`) only pre-checked the first RS. Fix: thread the full `rfp.requirementsSetIds` array as `suggestedRequirementsSetIds` (plural); mount block prefers the plural array over the singular id (cold-path AI Shopper entry — singular — still works). CombinedRequestModal's `useState(() => [...initialRequirementsSetIds])` initializer was already correct — bug was entirely upstream at the V2App handler + mount. New backlog **#202** filed: Remove "ACTOR" and "ASSET" small-caps labels from Detail Panels (pairs naturally with Phase 17.3 Claim Detail Panel content work). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1.2.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.2.1.1 — Hotfix: RFP-bound Asset + Claim greying + EA+DA notification Directory close + CombinedRequestModal RS scrollbox.** Four QA-driven items closing out the 17.2 arc against polish-backlog **#192** (Item J). Architectural correction: `assetId` is now a required field on `makeRfp` — every RFP binds to one of its owner's Assets at creation time, not at solicitation-accept time. All 118 seeded RFPs populated (Bob's Sentinel-4 RFP → `bAvionics` "Avionics Module"; 20 RFP-only mock-cluster owners each get one stub Asset; mixed-cluster owners reuse their first existing Asset). The Accept flow collapses Phase 17.2.1's two-modal AssetPickerModal → CombinedRequestModal chain into a single direct open of CombinedRequestModal pre-filled with the RFP's bound Asset; AssetPickerModal component file stays in the codebase for future create-RFP work (17.5+). SolicitationCreateModal greys out Claims already mapped to the RFP owner via an active or pending EA so the seller can't submit a solicitation pointing at a Claim that's already disclosed (which would error the buyer's Accept flow at the cold-path `already-disclosed` gate). EA+DA notification click handlers (`v22-request`, `v22-request-ea-only`, `v22-ea-accepted`, `v22-ea-declined`) close the Directory layer if open before cold-path navigation. CombinedRequestModal Step 1 RS scrollbox extended to all published Requirements Sets (network-wide) with globe icons + "Published by {owner}" labels — pre-selection via `initialRequirementsSetIds` (Accept flow) now works for any actor's published RS. RfpDetailPanel gains a "For Asset" row. Item J remaining scope: create-RFP entry point (17.5+), edit, withdraw/amend solicitations, multi-Claim solicitations, solicitor-side "open the resulting EA" deep-link via `acceptedEaId`. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1.1.

- 2026-05-19: **Phase 17.2.1 — RFP Accept flow / Request Agreement.** Closes the placeholder-disabled "Request Agreement" button that's been pending across the 17.2 + 17.2.0.x arc. The full RFP loop (post → discover → solicit → reject/accept → formalize EA+DA) is now functionally complete against polish-backlog **#192** (Item J). New `AssetPickerModal` component (single-select Asset list + context block + Continue gated on selection) takes the buyer through the architectural-rule step that requests must originate from one of the requester's Assets so the parent canvas can lay out the request-node + edge. Continue pre-fills the existing CombinedRequestModal via the same prop mechanism the AI Shopper uses — `requesterAsset` + `initialPin` (solicitor's Claim PIN) + `initialRequirementsSetIds` (first RFP RS id) — so **no CombinedRequestModal refactor was needed** (the stop-and-surface gate was passed cleanly). Submit-side effect lives inside `handleV22RequestSubmit` conditional on `v22AcceptingSolicitation`; cold-path behaviour is bit-identical. New `acceptSolicitation(solicitation, eaId)` pure transform + `acceptedEaId` field on `makeRfpSolicitation` link the accepted solicitation back to the resulting EA. New `v22-rfp-solicitation-accepted` notification (green ACCEPTED badge) joins the standard `v22-request` notification on the solicitor's inbox — dual notification by design (one informational pointer to the RFP/solicitation, one actionable pointer to the live EA+DA). SolicitationCard accepted-state treatment for both viewer modes (green badge, no action buttons, EA-pointer text). Architecture spec §8.9.6 added with full Accept-flow chain + dual notification rationale + cold-path preservation guarantee. Item J remaining scope: creation flow (17.6+), edit, withdraw / amend, multi-Claim solicitations, solicitor-side "open the resulting EA" deep-link via `acceptedEaId`. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.1.

- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.2.0.4 — LOD threshold revert + zoom control z-index + AI Shopper modal lag.** Three items, including the AI Shopper performance fix. (1) **LOD thresholds reverted** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: `MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` 2.5 → 3.4 (mini-cards 340% instead of 250%); `LOD_THRESHOLD` 5.5 → 5.0 (full-cards 500% instead of 550%); `MAX_ZOOM` stays at 6.5. The 17.2.0.3 lower bound put mini-cards into a regime where dense clusters (Pinnacle Systems et al.) had overlapping cards; 3.4 restores the screen-spacing > card-width invariant at the threshold while keeping a slightly-wider band than the pre-17.2.0.3 baseline. (2) **Directory zoom controls raised** from z=50 to z=1700 — sits between the card overlay (z=1500/1600) and the header pillbox (z=2000) / tooltip cards (z=2500) so cards no longer cover the +/-/FIT buttons. DOM-probe-verified: Directory's zoom controls are at the correct z=1700 with `data-v22-directory-layer` as ancestor; V2Canvas's parallel zoom controls remain at z=50 behind the Directory clip-path wipe as expected. (3) **AI Shopper modal lag fixed** — measured 81 ms click-to-render via puppeteer (down from ~5 s reported pre-fix). Two surgical fixes: module-level cache for `buildV22SharedArtifacts` in `v2_2Data.js` (320 ms cold → 0.001 ms cached); O(n²) → O(n) rewrite of the AI Shopper IIFE's public-claims derivation in V2App (`claimsById` Map replaces per-DA linear find; ~1.6 s → ~16 ms on seeded data). Benchmarked via standalone Node script. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.4.

- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.2.0.3 — RFP description in solicitation modal + expanded mini-card zoom range.** Two polish items batched before Phase 17.2.1's Accept-flow lift. (1) **RFP description in SolicitationCreateModal** — new section between the modal subtitle and the Required Standards accordion, rendering `rfp.description` as `white-space: pre-wrap` prose (multiline-preserving). Empty case falls back to muted-italic "No description provided." matching the RfpDetailPanel convention. Thin section divider below pairs with the existing divider between the accordion and Claim picker. (2) **Mini-card zoom range widened** in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: `MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` 3.333 → 2.5 (mini-cards now appear at 250% zoom, was 350%); `LOD_THRESHOLD` 4.375 → 5.5 (full-cards now appear at 550%, was 450%); `MAX_ZOOM` 5.0 → 6.5 so the new full-card threshold sits well below the cap with comparable headroom. Trade-off: the original density invariant (cards never overlap horizontally at the threshold) breaks at the low end of the band — mini-cards may visibly overlap inside dense clusters at 250% zoom. Accepted per the brief's "Andrew's judgement, readability wins" framing. `selectRfp`'s zoom override (Phase 17.2.0.2, `Math.max(zoomRef.current, LOD_THRESHOLD)`) automatically picks up the new 5.5 → notification clicks still land at full-card LOD. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.3.

- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.2.0.2 — Notification click completion + own-cluster label + RS accordion.** Closes the residual 17.2 notification-routing gap plus two polish items. (1) **Notification click completion** — Phase 17.2.0.1's `panToRfp` was pan-only at current zoom, so from the 15 % galactic-view default the camera barely moved and the on-canvas marker stayed unselected. Replaced with `selectRfp` (imperative handle), backed by a new `focusRfpInternal(d, dotIndex, { zoom })` helper shared between `handleMouseUp`'s RFP branch, `onCardClick`'s RFP branch, AND the external imperative path. Internal click paths keep current zoom (existing behaviour preserved); external `selectRfp` zooms up to `LOD_THRESHOLD` when starting below so the marker lands at full-card LOD. setPinned drives the on-canvas tooltip + select-state brightening, matching a manual click. (2) **Active actor's own-cluster label amber styling on the Directory layer.** PillboxLabel accepts a new `isOwn` boolean prop; when true, renders with `background: var(--accent-amber)` + `color: var(--bg-deep)`. Cluster label render block computes `isOwn = !!layout.activeParty && cluster.ownerParty === layout.activeParty`. Bob → GovCo, Alice → MicroCo, Dave → ChipCo all see their cluster styled; Carol/AuditCo (no cluster) gracefully degenerates. DOM-probe-verified at runtime: exactly 1 amber pill on Bob's view, 98 neutral. (3) **SolicitationCreateModal — Required Standards accordion** above the Claim picker. New `requirementsSets` prop (V2App threads `publishedRequirementSets`); new `RsAccordionEntry` sub-component renders each RS the RFP references as a clickable header (name + version pill + chevron) that expands to show its requirements list (id mono pill + label + description + criterion). Multi-expand via `Set<rsId>` state. Missing RS → "(Standard not found)"; zero RS IDs → "No required standards specified." 240 px max-height scrollbox; existing 90vh modal cap (ModalShared) handles small viewports. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.2.

- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.2.0.1 — Hotfix: notification routing + Directory cursor/hover + V2Canvas2 setState fix.** Three runtime-QA items from Phase 17.2 closed in one pass. (1) **Notification click routing** for `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` and `v22-rfp-solicitation-rejected` now fires the camera pan + zoom step (the 17.2 implementation registered the first three sub-steps — open Directory, clear Claim panel, set RFP panel — but omitted the pan to the RFP marker). New `useImperativeHandle`-exposed `panToRfp(rfp)` method on DirectoryLayer; V2App's notification handler retries via rAF until the Directory's phase machine reaches `in` and the layout is built (capped at 60 frames / ~1s — Directory opening transition is ~600 ms). (2) **Cursor pointer on Directory hover** for Claim dots, open RFP squares, closed-and-owned RFPs (mini-card / full-card hovers already work via AssetNode wrappers). The canvas's inline `cursor: default` style masked any container-level change; fix sets `canvasEl.style.cursor` imperatively from `handleMouseMove` based on raycast hit and resets on `onMouseLeave`. (3) **RFP open-marker hover/select brightening** now visibly renders. The 17.2 path wrote `instanceColor = (1,1,1)` against an indigo material color — the multiplier landed at identity, producing no visible delta. Fix: material color → white, base instance color = indigo, `flushRfpColors` lerps base 0.35 toward white on hover and 0.65 on select. Closed-RFP per-vertex colors get the same hover/select split (was: single 0.55 lerp for both). (4) **V2Canvas2 setState-in-render warning fixed** (root cause: `setV22DirectoryOpen` functional updater called `canvasRef.current.clearHoverState()` as a side effect inside the reducer, which ran during V2App's render and triggered V2Canvas's setState). Surgical lift-side-effect-out-of-updater fix; no structural refactor. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.0.1.

- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.2 — RFP solicitation flow (submit + deliver + reject loop).** Continued sub-phase against polish-backlog **#192** (Item J). Opens the seller→buyer engagement axis — first interactive write-path beyond the buyer-side lifecycle from 17.1. Sellers solicit one of their existing Claims against an open RFP from the RfpDetailPanel; the RFP owner sees incoming solicitations in a new section above the panel footer and can reject with an optional reply; the solicitor sees their own outgoing card and gets a notification on rejection. **New `makeRfpSolicitation`** factory in `v2_2Data.js` with `'pending' | 'rejected' | 'accepted'` status taxonomy (accepted reserved for Phase 17.2.1). **`mergeSolicitations(shared, solicitations)`** session-state overlay (mirror of `mergeProvisionals` / `mergeClosedRfps` — Map-backed). **V2App state**: `v22Solicitations: Map<id, RfpSolicitation>` + transient modal-open states; two handlers (`handleCreateSolicitation`, `handleRejectSolicitation`) build artifacts + fire notifications via the existing `enqueueV22NotificationForRequester` pattern. **Two new notification types**: `v22-rfp-solicitation-received` (to owner, amber badge) + `v22-rfp-solicitation-rejected` (to solicitor, red badge); both click-route to Directory → RFP marker → RfpDetailPanel and auto-dismiss. **Three new components**: `SolicitationCard` (status badge + message blocks + action bar — Request Agreement disabled with "Coming in Phase 17.2.1" tooltip; Reject enabled on pending), `SolicitationCreateModal` (Claim picker defaulting to zero selected per CLAUDE.md convention + 500-char optional message), `SolicitationRejectModal` (300-char optional reply + red destructive submit). **RfpDetailPanel three-way visibility branch**: owner sees "Incoming Solicitations (N)" + cards (empty case "No solicitations yet."); solicitor sees "Your Solicitation" + their card; other non-owner sees no section. Footer extensions: non-owner + open + no existing → "Solicit with my Claim"; non-owner + existing → muted "Already solicited — see above." **DirectoryLayer RFP marker hover + select brightening** (Items 19+20): per-instance `instanceColor` on rfpMesh + rfpFillMesh; per-vertex `color` attribute on closed-RFP LineDashedMaterial; new `flushRfpColors` callback parallel to `flushDotColors`. **Detail Panel 1px gap fix** (Item 21): both Directory-layer panel mount blocks bumped `bottom: 28` → `bottom: 27`. Architecture spec gains new §8.9 (RFP Solicitations) + §8 Changelog bullet. Forward queue: **Phase 17.2.1** — accept flow / Request Agreement (Asset-picker + CombinedRequestModal integration + provisional Claim node on buyer's parent canvas). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.2.
- 2026-05-18: **Phase 17.1 — RFP close/reopen lifecycle (owner-side).** First lifecycle phase against polish-backlog **#192** (Item J). Buyer-side state transitions for RFP nodes: the owner of an RFP can close it from the Detail Panel; closed RFPs render dashed on the owner's Directory at every LOD and are filtered out of every non-owner's Directory. Reopen restores the open state. **`makeRfp` factory** validates `status` against a `'open' | 'closed'` taxonomy and accepts `closedDate` (ISO string). **`closeRfp` / `reopenRfp`** pure transforms exported alongside `makeRfp`. **`mergeClosedRfps(shared, closedRfpIds)`** session-state overlay paralleling `mergeProvisionals`; storage is a `Map<rfpId, ISO closedDate>` so re-renders see stable timestamps. **`buildV22DirectoryDataForRole`** gains a 3rd `closedRfpIds` param; chains the merge internally and filters non-owner RFP lists on `status === 'open'` while keeping `ownRfps` unfiltered (asymmetric visibility). **V2App** holds `v22ClosedRfpIds` Map state next to `v22Provisionals`; threaded to DirectoryLayer; close/reopen handlers wired to the panel; selected RFP re-resolved from merged shared artifacts each render so the Detail Panel reflects current status. **RfpDetailPanel** gains a CLOSED status badge (muted treatment), a "Closed YYYY-MM-DD · HH:MM UTC" row, and an owner-only footer with a single direct-action button. **DirectoryLayer** partitions `rfpMesh` + `rfpFillMesh` on closed-owned (hidden); `rfpHitMesh` keeps all instances (closed still clickable for reopen); new `closedRfpMesh` (`THREE.LineSegments` + `LineDashedMaterial`) renders dashed outlines, geometry rebuilt on layout/zoom with `computeLineDistances()` after each rebuild. **AssetNode + AssetNodeMini** RFP branches read `node.isClosed` and switch `border-style: solid → dashed`. Architecture spec gains §8.8.7 (Lifecycle states + asymmetric visibility table + dashed-outline / dashed-card implementation + architectural rationale). Forward queue: Phase 17.2 (supplier solicitation + self-evaluation response — new `makeRfpResponse` factory + notification wiring), Phase 17.2.1 (buyer review + initiate formal EA), Phase 17.5+ (create-RFP entry point + post-and-edit lifecycle). Footer rolls forward to v0.17.1.
- 2026-05-17: **Phase 17.0.2 — Hotfix: TDZ crash in AssetNode RFP branch.** Phase 17.0.1's RFP early-return in `AssetNode` (full) referenced `hoverSelectColor` before its `const` declaration site (Phase 16.2.11 placed the declaration further down in the function body, after the new early-return). Every RFP card render and every RFP card click at mid/full LOD triggered `ReferenceError: Cannot access 'hoverSelectColor' before initialization`. Fix: hoist the declaration above the early-return. 3-line move; no semantic change for non-RFP renders. `AssetNodeMini` already had the declaration above its RFP branch — no change needed there. Swept both components for other TDZ-trapped vars; none surfaced. **Process note**: 17.0.1's structured review marked items as "Code-verified" for state-dependent render paths that should have been runtime-verified per CLAUDE.md. The bug would have been caught by a runtime walkthrough of "zoom to full-LOD on Bob's view" — which 17.0.1's QA explicitly didn't do (only verified full-LOD via a fiber-state-mutation probe that bypassed the React render path entirely). Future RFP/Claim render-path phases must runtime-verify zoom + hover + click at every LOD threshold per the brief's runtime-verification convention. Footer rolls forward to v0.17.0.2. Architecture spec gains a §8 Changelog bullet (no §8.8 content change — the RFP architecture from 17.0.1 is correct; only implementation order needed fixing).

Hollow-square outline + fill + hit-test meshes hide together at `zoom ≥ MID_LOD_THRESHOLD`. **`pinned` extended to a discriminated union** — exactly one of `claim` / `rfp` at any time. `setPinned` overwrites in a single atomic call so click swaps stay consistent. New `RfpTooltipCard` mirrors `ClaimTooltipCard` for the dot-LOD path; pinned-tooltip block branches on which discriminator is set. Empty-canvas click clears both discriminators (extends with `onRfpClick(null)` call). **V2App.jsx** minor: footer v0.17.0 → v0.17.0.1; Changelog modal entry prepended. **LOD parity with Claims is now complete.** Remaining #192 scope: Phase 17.1 (buyer post flow + `makeRfp` lifecycle metadata), Phase 17.2 (supplier discovery + self-evaluation response), Phase 17.2.1 (buyer review surface + initiate formal EA), Phase 17.5+ (create-RFP entry point + post-and-edit lifecycle). First sub-phase against #192's scope, partially addressing the "buyer-side review surface (RFP Detail Panel)" bullet — read-only portion. **Hit-test mesh**: new invisible solid-square `THREE.InstancedMesh` in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` parallel to the visible outline + fill meshes, matrices in lockstep via the existing populate + rescale-on-zoom paths. Panel mount shell mirrors the Claim Detail Panel. **Footer rolls forward to v0.17.0** — first forward-progress phase after the 16.2.11 footer freeze ended. Architecture spec gains new §8.8 RFP subsection + §8 Changelog bullet. **Out of scope** (deferred): RFP card LOD swap (17.0.1), RFP hover-preview / pinned tooltip (17.0.1), RS chip click behaviour (17.0.1), `makeRfp` lifecycle metadata (17.1 post flow), supplier self-evaluation response (17.2), buyer review + initiate formal EA (17.2.1), parent-canvas RFP rendering (later), Claim Detail Panel content polish (17.3), umbrella DA edges (17.4+), create-RFP entry point (17.5+). 118 seeded RFPs already in place from Phase 16.2.6.5 (Bob's Sentinel-4 + 20 RFP-only buyer mocks + 4 mixed actors).
- 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.11 — Phase 16 wrap-up: Alice grouping + footer version + parent-layer visual calibration.** Three loose ends closed before Phase 17 opens. **Item 1**: Alice per-Claim y-band grouping fix — `buildV22Canvas` chain anchor reads the evaluated Claim's y first (was `EA.granteeAssetId` Asset y per Phase 16.2.2). The granteeAssetId Asset isn't on grantor-direction views like Alice's, so chains were scattering via the symmetric-distribution pass-2 fallback. With the new anchor, each Claim's chain lives on the Claim's exact y (pass 1) or the nearest free row (pass 2). Bob's view unchanged. V2Canvas passes `disclosureType` to AssetNode/AssetNodeMini for Claim nodes; 16.2.10's branches now fire on parent. **Item 3b**: Non-Claim nodes (Actor, Asset, Eval Result, PoE) hover/select brightens to `var(--accent-indigo)` (was amber). New `hoverSelectColor` derived from `node.category` in both components, applied at three sites each (inner border, outer selection ring, inner-div hover background). Claims keep amber hover/select so the discrete state stays distinct from disclosure-type defaults. AssetNodeDot intentionally untouched (orthogonal concern). **Phase 16 (Directory Layer + parent-canvas visual calibration) is now CLOSED.** Forward queue: Phase 17.0 — RFP factory promotion + buyer post flow + clickable RFP dots + RFP Detail Panel. - 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.10 — Disclosure-type colored borders + tinted backgrounds on Directory Claim cards.** Adds the disclosure-type color signal (already present on dots since Phase 16.1.3 Item 8) to AssetNodeMini (mid-LOD) and AssetNode (full-LOD) cards on the Directory layer. A new `disclosureType` prop is plumbed through `DirectoryLayer.jsx` (cluster spec items → `placed` dots → `allDots` entries → card render block) and accepted by both card components in `AssetNode.jsx`. When `disclosureType` is `'full' | 'selective' | 'proofonly'` AND no higher-priority state applies (declined/revoked, provisional, hover/selected, bad-health), the card's border color signals the disclosure level (`var(--accent-indigo / amber / green)`) and a 12% `color-mix` tint of the same color washes the background. AssetNode (full) inserts the background branches ABOVE `isCounterpartyNode` in the priority chain — disclosure type is the more-specific signal. Parent canvas invocations of AssetNode/AssetNodeMini don't pass `disclosureType`, so parent-layer rendering is unchanged until parent wires it. Strict-equality conditionals (`disclosureType === 'full'` etc.) return falsy for `undefined` and fall through cleanly. RFP markers stay as hollow squares at every LOD (no card swap). Hover / select precedence preserved: hovering switches to amber, selection adds outer thick amber ring; disclosure-type color disappears under those states. **Priority #1 (Directory appearance and feel) is now complete.** Next forward-progress: priority #2 (Phase 17.0 — RFP factory promotion + buyer post flow + clickable RFP dots + RFP Detail Panel) or priority #3 (Claim Detail Panel verification + the deferred Detail Panel content items #58 / #104 / #116 / #161 / #180). 11 missed Changelog modal entries (16.2.6 → 16.2.10) prepended to the in-app modal as part of this phase. - 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.9 — Card-click pan + hover-preview offset fix.** Two surgical QA-driven fixes following Phase 16.2.8. **Item 1**: `onCardClick` in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` now appends the same `animatedPanToWithZoom(d.x + panelOffsetWorld, d.y, targetZoom, 500)` block the dot-click handler runs (Phase 16.2.7 omitted this on the wrong rationale that the user is "already viewing the card"). Without the pan, the Detail Panel opening on the right partially covers the just-clicked card. Same 500 ms ease, `targetZoom = zoomRef.current` (no zoom change). `animatedPanToWithZoom` added to the `useCallback` deps. **Item 2**: `AssetNodeMini`'s portal-tooltip transform in `AssetNode.jsx` (line ~1534) changed from `translate(-50%, -50%) scale(0.85)` to `translate(calc(-50% + ${(ACTION_BAR_W * 0.85) / 2}px), -50%) scale(0.85)`. The wrapper is `CARD_W + ACTION_BAR_W = 244 px` wide; without the shift, the visible card body's centre lands 14.45 px LEFT of `tooltipPos.x` (the mini-card's amber hover border peeks out on the right of the preview, obvious on Directory with unscaled 160 px mini-cards). The translate is applied in post-scale visual px so the shift value is `(ACTION_BAR_W × 0.85) / 2 = 14.45 px`. Subtly improves parent-canvas mini-LOD alignment too — the offset has always been there, just masked by the mini-card's zoom-driven scale. No changes to `AssetNodeDot`'s tooltip (different render path, no issue flagged). No changes to RFP rendering, cluster labels, mock data, or other Directory state. **Build clean**; **no console errors** on dev server. - 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.8 — Directory Claim card population (CLAIM/minibar/badges).** Phase 16.2.7 shipped the LOD swap by passing raw `claim` as the `node` prop, leaving cards visually bare (title + owner only). This phase routes Directory Claims through `claimToNode` + new mock distribution helpers so the cards now read identically to parent-canvas Claim cards. New helpers in `v2_2Data.js` near `claimToNode`: `mockClaimHealth(claimId)` returns a `{ ok, warn, bad }` rollup keyed by claim.id via `seededRandom(hashString(claimId + ':health'))`; `mockClaimBadges(claimId, health)` returns `[{ id, badgeName, badgeVersion, issuerParty }]` only when `health.bad === 0 && health.warn === 0 && health.ok > 0`. Distribution per brief: 50% no minibar, 5% all-green ≥ 2 badges, 5% all-green 1 badge, 10% all-green no badges, 15% has-bad (red border), 15% mixed (ok+warn). Inline `MOCK_BADGE_NAMES` (12 standards: ISO 27001, SOC 2 Type II, ITAR Compliance, DO-178C Level A, EN 9100, AS9100D, CMMC Level 3, NIST 800-171, FIPS 140-3, DO-254 Level B, MIL-STD-810H, RTCA DO-160G) and `MOCK_BADGE_ISSUERS` (AuditCo + 4 mock audit-style orgs). Badge shape matches `BadgeChipContainer.jsx`'s consumer contract verbatim (confirmed by reading `ShieldTooltipContent` + `OverflowTooltipContent`). View-builder plumb: `buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` builds `cluster.nodesByClaimId` Maps per other cluster (covering both publicClaims + umbrellaClaims) and a top-level `ownNodesByClaimId` Map for the active actor — each Map value is `claimToNode(claim, { health, claimCount: 0 }, 0, 0)` with `_activeBadges` attached. DirectoryLayer plumb: `buildItems` reads from these maps and threads `node` onto each spec item; `computeLayout`'s `placed` dot construction includes the new `node` field; `allDots.push` propagates it; the JSX card overlay block uses `node={d.node || d.claim}` (raw fallback). **Click flow preserved**: `onCardClick` and dot-click both continue passing raw `d.claim` to `onClaimDotClick` so the Detail Panel still receives the shape it expects (raw Claim has `referencedAssetIds` / `acknowledgments` / `pin`; the enriched node has `referencedEvidenceIds`, which would break the panel). **Probe verified** across 22,994 enriched nodes on Bob's view: 49.9%/20.0%/15.2%/15.0% distribution; 10.0% with ≥ 1 badge; 4.9% with ≥ 2 badges. Determinism check passes (same claim.id → same health + badges across reloads). **Dev server verified** at zoom 5.0 (full-LOD): cards show CLAIM teal label at top, owner-actor below, minibars (~half of cards) with all-green / mixed / has-bad distributions, red borders on has-bad cards, badge chips on a subset of all-green cards. No console errors. No changes to AssetNode.jsx, BadgeChipContainer.jsx, RFP rendering, dot Three.js mesh, or `makeClaim` output. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 / 16.2.6.6 / 16.2.7 defensive settings preserved. - 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.7 — Mini-card + full-card LOD on Directory + size tweak.** Three coordinated changes in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` that close polish-backlog #200 and tighten low-zoom rendering. (1) **Size tweak**: `BASE_DOT_FACTOR` 0.85 → 0.70, `BASE_RFP_FACTOR` 0.95 → 0.80. Linear-regime marker sizes shrink ~16-18% at zoom 15-30%; cap-engaged appearance unchanged at zoom ≥ ~0.65. (2) **`MAX_ZOOM` lifted** 1.5 → 5.0 — makes room for two LOD swap thresholds without overlap. (3) **LOD swap for Claim dots only**: at zoom ≥ `MID_LOD_THRESHOLD = MINI_CARD_W / DOT_GRID = 3.333…`, dots replaced by `AssetNodeMini` HTML overlays; at zoom ≥ `LOD_THRESHOLD = CARD_W / DOT_GRID = 4.375` they swap to full `AssetNode`. Both card components reused verbatim from `AssetNode.jsx` (brief's hard rule: NO modifications to that file). **Density invariant**: cards render at FIXED NATURAL PIXEL SIZE (no `transform: scale(${zoom})`) — at the threshold, screen-space inter-dot spacing exactly equals card width (3.333 × 48 = 160 = MINI_CARD_W; 4.375 × 48 = 210 = CARD_W) so cards never overlap horizontally. Viewport-cull skips cards outside `viewport + cardW/cardH` buffer on each edge. Implementation: (a) new render block in JSX between RFP-owner-label and tooltip blocks; (b) `useEffect` toggles `dotsMesh.visible = zoom < MID_LOD_THRESHOLD`; (c) `useEffect` clears `hover` when crossing into mid-LOD (raycast against hidden mesh yields no hits); (d) tooltip render gated on `zoom < MID_LOD_THRESHOLD` (cards have their own hover behavior); (e) new `onCardClick` mirrors dot click flow but skips animated pan/zoom (user can see the card they clicked). `pinned` survives LOD transitions so the relevant card / dot renders `isSelected={true}`. **RFPs stay as hollow squares** at all LODs — RFP card swap deferred to a future phase paired with RFP Detail Panels. **Cluster pillbox labels stay as `PillboxLabel`** at all LODs. Card dimension constants (`CARD_W = 210`, `CARD_H = 96`, `MINI_CARD_W = 160`, `MINI_CARD_H = 48`) mirrored in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` because `AssetNode.jsx` doesn't export them — documented as a sync'd duplicate so future changes stay coordinated. **Verified** on dev server (1400×900 viewport, Bob's view): at zoom 366% (mid-LOD), 23 AssetNodeMini cards rendered with non-overlapping spacing. At zoom 500% (full-LOD), AssetNode full cards visible. Dot mesh hidden in both regimes. **Polish-backlog #200 closed** by this phase (moved to Completed section). Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 / 16.2.6.6 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention. Architecture spec gains §8.2 Phase 16.2.7 changelog bullet.
- 2026-05-17: **Phase 16.2.6.6 — RFP marker scaling + tinted fill.** QA on the 16.2.6.5 cluster expansion surfaced four related issues: RFP squares oversized at high zoom (no cap, vs dots' 22 px on-screen cap), borders proportionally tiny on oversized squares, adjacent grid-cell squares overlapping each other (1.2 × DOT_GRID outer extended past cell boundary), and markers reading as empty rectangles with no fill. All four fixed in one pass. Six items in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`. **Item 1**: new constants `BASE_RFP_FACTOR = 0.95`, `MAX_SCREEN_RFP_PX = 22`, `RFP_FILL_ALPHA = 0.15`, `RFP_BASE_OUTER = DOT_GRID × 0.95 = 45.6 wu`. Old `RFP_OUTER_SIZE` kept as backwards-compat alias for label-positioning formulas. New `computeRfpWorldSize(zoom)` paralleling `computeDotWorldSize` — cap threshold ≈ 0.48 (sooner than dots' 0.54 because RFP base factor is higher). **Item 2**: outline geometry rebuilt at `makeHollowSquareGeometry(RFP_BASE_OUTER, RFP_BORDER)`. **Item 3**: new `rfpFillMeshRef` + companion `THREE.InstancedMesh` with `PlaneGeometry(RFP_BASE_OUTER, RFP_BASE_OUTER)` + indigo `MeshBasicMaterial({ transparent: true, opacity: 0.15 })`. Added to scene BEFORE the outline mesh so the outline draws on top of the fill. Disposal added to cleanup. **Item 4**: populate loop updated — per-instance matrix bakes scale via `m.makeScale(s, s, 1); m.setPosition(...)`, writes to BOTH outline and fill meshes per instance. **Item 5**: new `rfpScaleRef` + rescale-on-zoom `useEffect` parallel to the dot rescale effect. Updates both meshes' matrices when scale changes by ≥ 1e-4. Effect declared BEFORE the border-rebuild effect so `rfpScaleRef.current` is up-to-date when read. **Item 6**: border-rebuild formula updated to `RFP_BORDER_SCREEN_PX / (scale × zoom)` — reduces to the pre-16.2.6.6 formula below the cap; constant above the cap. **Verified**: 99 PillboxLabel elements + 118 RFP markers rendered correctly on Bob's view at default zoom 0.15. Dot rendering unchanged from 16.2.6.5. Mixed clusters preserve the dots-inner-squares-outer pattern with the new sizing. No new console errors. Phase 16.1.4 / 16.1.5 / 16.2.3 / 16.2.6.1 / 16.2.6.4 / 16.2.6.5 defensive settings preserved. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention. Each `makeActor` with `role: 'buyer'`; no Claims/Assets/DAs. Each runs through both `seedMockSupplierActor` and `generateRfpsForActor`. (3) **New exported `generateRfpsForActor(actor, count)` helper** using existing `hashString` + `seededRandom` (mulberry32) seeded with `${actor.party}:rfps:${count}` for determinism. 6 templates × component/mission/program dictionaries → procedural RFP names. (4) **`buildV22DirectoryDataForRole` extended**: new `PRIMARY_PARTIES_FOR_ORPHAN_RFP` set; every cluster now carries `cluster.rfps`; RFP-only owners upsert as new clusters except primary parties; `otherRfps` narrowed to orphan-only (preserves 16.2.6.3 GovCo-on-non-Bob orphan-label path). (5) **`computeLayout` updates**: `buildItems` now also returns `rfpItems` from `cluster.rfps`; new `ACTOR_KIND` taxonomy (ACTIVE / CLAIMS_CLUSTER / RFP_CLUSTER / MIXED_CLUSTER); initial seed positions branch on kind (RFP-only bottom-bias [0.70, 0.94] × usable height, mixed cross-zone [0.60, 0.70], Claims upper [0.10, 0.65]); target-area formula extended with `RFP_AREA_FACTOR = 1.44`; hard y-clamp inside Lloyd's loop pins RFP-only seeds to `≥ RFP_ZONE_TOP_THRESHOLD`. (6) **Per-RFP-marker label suppression**: added `if (d.clusterIdx !== -1) return null` guard so only orphan RFPs render a per-marker label. **Probe verified**: actors 77→101 (+24), claims 22,824→22,994 (+170), assets 22,821→22,991 (+170), DAs 91,321→92,001 (+680), rfps 1→118 (+117). Procedural RFP names deterministic across reloads. **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief pinned conventions**: cluster overflow grew 18 → 20 clusters at the new seed density (Bowsprit Defense + Andromeda Defense newly join the list, with Apex Avionics's placement dropping further from 225 → 143 of 325 dots). - 2026-05-16: **Phase 16.2.6.4 — Label z-order fix (smaller clusters on top).** Single-item phase resolving the label-overlap issues surfaced after 16.2.6.3's 25-actor expansion: ChipCo buried behind Prism Aerospace, MicroCo behind Precision Components, Photonix truncated to "Photo..." behind Substrate Dynamics. Two new module-level constants in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`: `Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL = 100` + `Z_RFP_LABEL = 1100`. `PillboxLabel` accepts a new `zIndex` prop (default 4) applied to the absolutely-positioned outer container. Cluster-label render block sorts `layout.allClusters` by `dots.length` descending once per render, builds a party→rank Map, and threads `zIndex={Z_BASE_CLUSTER_LABEL + rank}` to each PillboxLabel — largest cluster gets lowest z, smallest cluster gets highest z. RFP owner-actor labels (16.2.6.3 Item 4) get `zIndex={Z_RFP_LABEL}` so they always render above every cluster label. Sort stability handles `dotCount` ties; empty-cluster active actors (Bob/GovCo, Carol/AuditCo) land at the top of the small end of the sort. **Trade-off accepted per brief**: large clusters' label pills may have corners clipped when adjacent to small ones, but the larger cluster's identity is conveyed by its visual footprint (Helios at 3,250 dots is unmistakable regardless of pill clipping). DOM probe on Bob's view confirms Helios (3,250 dots) at z=101, descending through Photonix at z=172, MicroCo at z=173, GovCo at z=174 — z inversely correlates with dotCount as intended. On Alice's view the GovCo RFP-marker owner-label renders at z=1100, above every cluster label. No regression: positioning, font-size scaling, and content unchanged from 16.2.6.3. - 2026-05-16: **Phase 16.2.6.2 — Edge buffers + zoom range + dot scaling + wipe origin.** Four surgical polish items addressing post-16.2.6.1 QA. (1) **Voronoi-domain insets**: four new constants in `DirectoryLayer.jsx` (`DOMAIN_INSET_LEFT/RIGHT = DOT_GRID`, `DOMAIN_INSET_TOP = 500`, `DOMAIN_INSET_BOTTOM = 250` world units) shrink the tessellation rectangle inward — 1-cell edge buffer on left/right, world-space equivalent of the app header (~61 css px) + footer (~32 css px) at MIN_ZOOM = 0.15 on top/bottom. Both `delaunay.voronoi(...)` call sites pass the inset bounds; cells are clipped at those edges so dots can't land in the inset regions. Lloyd's target-sum check moved to compare against `usableArea` instead of full canvas (ratio now ~0.78). (2) **`MAX_ZOOM` 4.0 → 1.5**: paired with the new dot-size cap; beyond 150% no useful new detail until the Directory mini-card swap (deferred — filed as #200) ships. (3) **Non-linear dot scaling**: new `BASE_DOT_FACTOR = 0.85` (was 0.95) + `MAX_SCREEN_DOT_PX = 22` + new helper `computeDotWorldSize(zoom)` returning world diameter that yields `min(linear, 22 px)` on screen. Cap threshold ≈ 0.54 zoom. Per-instance matrix bakes scale via `m.makeScale(s, s, 1); m.setPosition(d.x, -d.y, 0)` — NOT mesh-level scale (which would also move positions). New zoom-change `useEffect` rewrites all instance matrices when the scale changes; `dotScaleRef` short-circuits no-op rewrites. `DOT_RADIUS` adjusted to `DOT_GRID × BASE_DOT_FACTOR / 2` (= 20.4). (4) **Wipe origin** default moved from bottom-left (`'0% 100%'`) to bottom-center (`'50% 100%'`); pre-pinned wipeOrigin from Phase 11.8 #44's node-double-click flow still wins, so only the chrome-globe-click default path changes. - 2026-05-16: **Phase 16.2.6.1 — Dense organic cluster packing + ~22k dot expansion.** Three coordinated changes that resolve Phase 16.2.6's QA trio (spiral gaps inside clusters, half-empty top 75% of canvas, super-jumbo sunflower overflow) via a single algorithmic shift: **dense Voronoi-clipped grid fill** replaces Vogel sunflower in `DirectoryLayer.jsx`. Each cluster's Voronoi polygon is shrunk inward by 1 DOT_GRID, every grid cell inside the shrunken polygon is enumerated (label hole excluded), sorted by distance from centre, and the first N filled. New helpers `packClusterDense` + `shrinkConvexPolygon` (per-vertex bisector miter, orientation-detected); existing `pointInPolygon` reused. **Removed** Vogel sunflower primitives (`vogelPosition`, `GOLDEN_ANGLE`, `SUNFLOWER_SCALE`, `SUNFLOWER_MAX_ITER_PER_DOT`, the per-pair `INTER_CLUSTER_BUFFER` check, `isInLabelHole`). The 2-cell inter-cluster gap is now intrinsic to the algorithm (both neighbours shrink 1 cell). **Lloyd's target area** swapped from `canvasArea × (dots / totalDots)` (proportional but area-blind) to `(dots × DOT_GRID² + LABEL_HOLE_AREA) × 1.20` (physically grounded — per-dot area + label hole + 20% packing/wobble overhead). Target-sum-vs-canvas-area surface added — warns if sum > canvas area. At the new seed the ratio is 0.73 (within the 70–80% brief target). **35 mock supplier dot counts expanded ~6.5×** in `v2_2Data.js`'s `PHASE_16_2_6_NEW_MOCK_ACTORS` (Helios 500 → 3250, Atlas 450 → 2950, Polaris 400 → 2600, Vortex 350 → 2300 down to Cordite Labs 5 → 35). Same 35 actors, same verticals, same procedural Claim pipeline. New totals: Directory now ~21,609 dots (21,435 new mock + 157 existing mock + 17 switchable). Delta from 16.2.6: +18,147 Claims, +18,147 stub Assets, +72,588 Disclosure Agreements (3 internal own/ref + 1 public per new Claim). `DOT_RADIUS` bumped `DOT_GRID × 0.425 → DOT_GRID × 0.475` so diameter = `DOT_GRID × 0.95` and adjacent grid-aligned dots butt with a ~5% gap. `MAX_DOTS` bumped 10000 → 25000 for InstancedMesh capacity. **Defensive fixes** (per brief's pinned conventions): shrink-collapse fallback for narrow Voronoi cells (`packClusterDense` reverts to the unshrunken cell with a `console.warn` instead of producing zero dots — ChipCo's 14-dot cluster hits this case at the current Lloyd's convergence); finite-vertex filter on the umbrella SVG path render (drops umbrella paths with NaN screenPoints, which was producing "NaN is an invalid value for left" React warnings during the brief initial-mount race window). **Known regressions surfaced + accepted per brief, filed as 16.2.6.2 candidates**: (a) Lloyd's residual still 161 wu at the 20-iter cap (≈ 3.4 × DOT_GRID) — brief explicitly forbids unilaterally raising the cap; (b) cluster overflow warnings remain on 9 clusters at the new counts — Helios 789/3250, Atlas 468/2950, Polaris 300/2600, Vortex 854/2300, Vega 946/1200, Aurora 620/800, Beacon 520/900, Solstice 268/700, Apex 202/325; (c) ChipCo's tiny cell collapses on shrink (falls back to unshrunken). The visual remains the brief's intent: top 75% of canvas densely populated with organic, varied cluster shapes; label holes preserved; inter-cluster buffer clearly visible; super-jumbos visibly massive. Phase 16.1.4 `shouldMountScene` + Phase 16.1.5 InstancedMesh defensive settings + Phase 16.2.3 camera-init NaN guard all preserved. Phase 16.2.6's brief vs. table arithmetic-error caveat (3,328 vs 3,288 baseline) still applies retrospectively. **Next phase queue updated**: 16.2.6.2 (super-jumbo Voronoi cell sizing + Lloyd's convergence tuning + ChipCo cell allocation if visual reveals tessellation issues), then 16.2.7 (Alice per-Claim grouping follow-up to 16.2.2), then Phase 17.0–17.2 (RFP MVP). Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention. Architecture spec gains §8.2 Phase 16.2.6.1 changelog bullet.

Heavy-tailed by design — 4 super-jumbo clusters carry ~50% of the new dot mass: Helios Industries 500, Atlas Avionics 450, Polaris Defense 400, Vortex Aerospace 350; long tail of medium and small clusters for variety. **Procedural Claim names**: new `VERTICAL_LEXICONS` constant in `v2_2Data.js` covering 21 verticals (20 hand-defined per brief Item 2 + `'Connectors & Backshells'` filled in to cover row-20's gap between Item 1 and Item 2). - 2026-05-16: **Phase 16.2.5 — Grid alignment + dot rendering hotfix.** Three constant changes that reconcile cluster dot positions with the background dot matrix and make dots readable at the default 15% zoom. **`DOT_GRID` 12 → 48** — matches the background grid's actual world-space spacing (`GRID_SPACING`, which was `4×DOT_GRID`). Every sunflower-snapped dot position now lands on a background-grid intersection. `GRID_SPACING` redefined as `DOT_GRID` directly so the relationship is structural; background grid origin snapped to a multiple of `GRID_SPACING` so grid points pass through `(0, 0)`. **`DOT_RADIUS` 3 → `DOT_GRID × 0.425` (≈20.4 wu)** — dots fill ~85% of their grid cell. Was barely visible at 15% zoom (0.45 screen px); now ~6 screen px at default zoom. **`SUNFLOWER_SCALE` 1.7 → 1.0** — Vogel arm spacing matches grid. Combined effect: pixelated sunflower clusters that read as solid filled discs around the centered label hole at default zoom. **Derived constants** scale automatically: `LABEL_HOLE_W = 288`, `LABEL_HOLE_H = 144`, `INTER_CLUSTER_BUFFER = 96`, `LLOYD_CONVERGENCE_DELTA = 48`. **Lloyd's convergence**: at the current 12-actor seed, max displacement after 10 iterations is 193.8 wu (4× the new 48-wu threshold; was 16× the old 12-wu threshold). Per the brief, accepted with `console.warn` rather than raising the cap — Phase 16.2.6's denser seed will re-test. **Brief Item 2 caveat**: the brief's "cluster footprint shrinks ~2.9×" calculation assumed `DOT_GRID` stayed at 12; with `DOT_GRID = 48`, footprints actually grow ~2.4× while dot size grows 6.8× — net visual reads as denser (the intent). Phase queue updated: 16.2.6 (seed expansion to ~3k dots) next, then 16.2.7 (Alice per-Claim grouping follow-up). - 2026-05-16: **Phase 16.2.4 — Directory galactic view v2 (Voronoi packing + sunflower clusters).** Andrew's feedback on Phase 16.2.3: the polar Poisson disc fan-out left the canvas feeling sparse; emptiness reads as a negative for the "wow" view. Phase 16.2.4 rebuilds the Directory's spatial primitives end-to-end. **Smaller canvas**: 17280×11170 → 11520×7447 (16" MBP logical at 15% default zoom). `MIN_ZOOM` / `INITIAL_ZOOM` 0.1 → 0.15. Same seed density, smaller area = denser-feeling. **Lloyd-iterated centroidal Voronoi tessellation** (replacing polar Poisson disc): active Actor's seed pinned at the bottom-center anchor (index 0); other seeds at hash-derived deterministic positions; Lloyd's iteration pulls each non-pinned seed toward area-weighted centroid via `stepFactor = 0.5 + 0.5*tanh(area_error)`. Iteration cap 10. Cells target area ∝ dot_count_i / total_dots × canvas_area. **Sunflower clusters** (replacing rectangular row/col grid): Vogel phyllotaxis — `angle = i × GOLDEN_ANGLE`, `radius = sqrt(i + 0.5) × DOT_GRID × 1.7` — around each Voronoi cell centroid, with a reserved 6×3 cell `LABEL_HOLE` at the center for the Actor label. Exclusion checks: inside label hole → skip; outside Voronoi cell → skip; within `2 × DOT_GRID` of another cluster's dot → skip; within `DOT_GRID × 0.9` of an own cluster dot → skip. Umbrella items placed first so they sit in the inner spiral arcs. **Centered Actor label**: PillboxLabel HTML overlay moved from above-grid to dead-center on the cluster centroid (`transform: translate(-50%, -50%)`). The Three.js Actor-squares InstancedMesh is kept (scene lifecycle symmetry) but rendered with `count=0`. **Umbrella outline path**: convex hull of umbrella dots offset outward by 1 DOT_GRID via Andrew's monotone-chain + per-vertex bisector miter. Fallbacks for <3 umbrella dots: bounding circle / capsule. Stroked in `var(--accent-amber)` 1.5px + 8% amber fill — reverts the Phase 16.1.3 grey treatment per the brief. The legacy `CLUSTER_PAD`-based L-shape construction removed. **Loading animation v2**: `playDirectoryLoadAnimation` extended with `umbrellaOutlines` param + `setUmbrellaOpacity` callback. Each outline fades 0 → 1 in sync with its cluster label (start time = `distFromAnchor / waveSpeed + labelDelayMs/1000`). `skip()` snaps dot + label + outline opacities to 1. **d3-delaunay added as dependency** (`^6.0.4`). **Lloyd's convergence caveat**: at the current 12-actor seed, max displacement after 10 iterations is ~194 wu (above the 12-wu convergence threshold); per the brief's "stop and surface, don't raise the cap unilaterally" rule, we accept those positions and emit a `console.warn`. The visual is acceptable for the prototype demo and Phase 16.2.5 (seed expansion to ~3k dots) will re-test convergence on a denser dataset. **#196 (force-directed Directory layout) stays Closed** — superseded by the Lloyd+sunflower pipeline. Phase 16.1.4 `shouldMountScene` + Phase 16.1.5 `frustumCulled = false` / unbounded `boundingSphere` defensive settings intact. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention. Architecture spec gains §8.2 Phase 16.2.4 changelog bullet.
- 2026-05-13: **Phase 16.2.3 — Directory galactic view + loading animation.** `DirectoryLayer.jsx` rewritten to use a bounded 17280×11170 design surface (matching 16" MBP logical resolution at 10% zoom) + radial polar fan-out + radial wave loading animation. **Bounded canvas + new zoom**: `MIN_ZOOM` lowered 0.5 → 0.1; `INITIAL_ZOOM` set to 0.1; on first load and every role-switch re-entry the camera resets to `(CANVAS_WIDTH/2, CANVAS_HEIGHT/2)` at zoom 0.1, showing the full 17280×11170 canvas in an MBP viewport. Zoom-percentage display now maps directly (zoom × 100): "10%", "100%", "400%". **Active Actor anchor** at `(8640, 8936)` — canvas-bottom-center, 20% up from the bottom edge. Carol (anonymous, no own cluster) still uses the same anchor as the polar origin. **Radial polar fan-out** replaces the deterministic-bbox-overlap 30-attempt up-zigzag from Phase 16.2: each non-active cluster picks `(θ, r)` via Poisson disc sampling — θ ∈ [-75°, +75°] from straight up (upper 150° arc), r ∈ [2000, 8377.5] world units. Sort input by descending Claim count so jumbo clusters get first pick. Up to 50 retries with seed-perturbed samples; collision check via the existing 12-cell buffer; canvas-bounds check `bbox ⊆ [0,W]×[0,H]`. Fallback after 50 retries logs a `console.warn` and keeps the last candidate. **Pan-bounds recompute per zoom**: `clampPan` derives bounds from `(CANVAS_WIDTH/HEIGHT, viewport, zoom)` on every call — at zoom=0.1 bounds collapse to a single point (full canvas already fits); at higher zoom they open up so the user can traverse the canvas without revealing void beyond it. **FIT** recalculates zoom from `min(viewport.w/CANVAS_WIDTH, viewport.h/CANVAS_HEIGHT)` and centers camera on canvas-center. **Loading animation** (new helper `src/v2/directoryLoadAnimation.js`, parallel to `edgeDrawIn.js`'s pattern): cluster dots fade in via a radial wave emanating from the active Actor anchor on every Directory entry (initial mount + role switch); per-dot `t_start = d / waveSpeed` (3000 world units / sec); ramp opacity 0 → 1 over 200ms; labels fade in 100ms after the first dot in their cluster appears; total duration ~3s. Dot opacity is realised via per-instance color multiplication on the existing InstancedMesh — the Directory background is opaque dark so scaling color → 0 makes the dot disappear into `--bg-deep` cleanly without needing a custom shader. Click on empty canvas during animation calls `skip()` and snaps everything to 1.0; dot clicks open the Detail Panel normally and do NOT skip; pan / zoom run independently of the wave. **Camera-init NaN guard** added: `updateCamera` bails when container width/height ≤ 0 (briefly true during initial mount), preventing a zero-width orthographic frustum from producing NaN projection-matrix entries that propagate through `worldToScreen` and break HTML overlay positioning; `PillboxLabel` additionally short-circuits when its `x` / `y` aren't finite. **#196 (force-directed Directory layout) closed** — superseded by the polar Poisson disc fan-out. Phase 16.1.5's `frustumCulled = false` + unbounded `boundingSphere` on every InstancedMesh left intact; Phase 16.1.4's `shouldMountScene` lifecycle left intact. Footer stays at v0.16.2.0 per backtrack-hotfix convention. Architecture spec gains §8.2 Phase 16.2.3 changelog bullet.
- 2026-05-13: **Phase 16.2.2 — Parent canvas node spreading.** Refactor of `buildV22Canvas` in `src/v2/v2_2Data.js` (the parent-canvas layout function that assigns `x, y` to every node before V2Canvas consumes them). **Three coordinated changes**: (1) **chain-row coalescing**: each evaluation chain (one row per `chainOrigin`) is now y-anchored to its evaluator's `EA.granteeAssetId` Asset on canvas via a two-pass allocator. Pass 1 — first chain at each anchor claims the anchor's `y` exactly. Pass 2 — subsequent chains pick the nearest non-colliding `y` via symmetric outward search. Chain successors share their origin's y so re-runs read as a single horizontal row. Falls back to symmetric distribution when the anchor isn't on canvas. (2) **Increased grantee-direction x-gap**: `COL_PULLED_CLAIM` bumped 2100 → 2400 (with `COL_PULLED_ASSET` 2500 → 2800, `COL_PUBLIC` 2900 → 3200, `COL_OWN_POE` 2100 → 2400) so the gap between rightmost chain node (owned PoE at 2000 for non-supersession chains) and the pulled Claim is 400 world units — comfortably above the brief's ≥240 minimum. Previous 100-unit gap read as visual overlap on Bob/Carol/Dave's parent canvases. (3) **PoE column order on grantor-direction views**: `proofOfEvalPulledPoEs` placement moved from `COL_OWN_EVAL_eff - ER_COL_SPACING` (between Claim and ER, tight 100-unit gap to Claim) to `COL_OWN_EVAL_eff + ER_COL_SPACING` (right of ER, matching the brief's `Claim → ER → PoE → counterparty Asset` order). **Proof-only-pulled PoE y** now inherits from the wrapped ER's `y` for chain-row alignment on Dave-style views. **Architectural deviation surfaced (per the brief's escape clause)**: both Bob's evaluations carry `granteeAssetId: bAvionics`, so the brief's "bAvionics for PRM eval, bThermal for VReg eval" example doesn't hold — strict alignment is impossible when two chains share an anchor; the two-pass allocator stacks them symmetrically. **Verification**: zero node-overlap pairs across all four role views via probe (≈240×100 bounding-box check). The Directory now hosts 12 new mock supplier Actors representing the defense-electronics supply chain under Bob's Sentinel-4 satellite program: NovaFab (25 Claims), ElectroGrid (24), Precision Components (18), AvionicSys (17), Substrate Dynamics (16), Helix RF (11), Optech Sensors (10), SolarVantage (10), ThermaCore (9), CompoStruct (9), Photonix (4), Cryotek (4). Companion `pickDirectoryType(i, total)` interleaver assigns disclosure types per Claim to target ~60% full / 25% selective / 15% proofonly so every cluster paints a visible indigo + amber + green mix; small clusters (n ≤ 4) get a forced fixed pattern so Photonix and Cryotek stay non-monotone. **DirectoryLayer placement-scaling patch**: the initial cluster x-spread band scales with cluster count `N` (`xSpread = Math.max(800, 600 × (N − 1))`) instead of the previous hardcoded 800-px band. With 14 non-own clusters on Bob's view the initial band is ~7800 px — comfortably enough that the 30-attempt up-zigzag retry no longer needs to compensate. **Status of #196 (force-directed layout) unchanged — still Open**: the cheap x-spread patch covers the N=14 scale documented in the brief; #196 remains the canonical follow-up if QA surfaces overlaps at higher N (or if cluster shapes become more variable). Phase 16.1.5's defensive `frustumCulled = false` + unbounded `boundingSphere` settings on every `InstancedMesh` left intact; Phase 16.1.4's `shouldMountScene` lifecycle pattern left intact. Footer stays v0.16.1.5 (no behavior change).
- 2026-05-10: **Phase 16.1.5 — Hotfix: Frustum culling + raycast bounding-sphere fix.** Two QA issues from 16.1.4, both downstream of the lifecycle change: (1) dots disappeared at zoom > ~60%; (2) dot clicks no longer opened the Detail Panel. **Shared root cause**: `THREE.InstancedMesh.boundingSphere` is auto-computed from underlying geometry vertices (which sit at origin because instance positions live in per-instance matrices). The initial computed sphere is tiny (radius = `DOT_RADIUS = 3`, centred at origin) and gets cached. Both the renderer's frustum check (`Frustum.intersectsObject`) and the raycast pre-filter (`InstancedMesh.raycast` → `raycaster.ray.intersectsSphere(_sphere)`) read this cached sphere — the tiny origin-sphere fell outside the camera frustum at high zoom and outside click rays at any zoom, so the entire mesh was rejected before per-instance work could run. Phase 16.1.4 stabilized the scene lifecycle but didn't touch bounding-sphere caching, so both symptoms surfaced once the lifecycle stopped masking them. **Fix**: on each InstancedMesh (dots + Actor squares + RFP), set `frustumCulled = false` and override `boundingSphere = new THREE.Sphere(origin, Infinity)`. The former bypasses the renderer's frustum check; the latter forces the raycast pre-filter to always pass so per-instance raycast can run. **Item 2 note**: the brief's alternate "closure staleness in scene-init listeners" diagnosis did not match actual code — mouse handlers in DirectoryLayer.jsx are React JSX props bound via `useCallback` with full deps, not `addEventListener` calls inside a long-lived effect. No handler refactor was required; the bounding-sphere fix addresses the click symptom via the shared raycast pre-filter. Verified: dots render at zoom factor 4.0 (UI 100%) when camera is on a cluster (wheel-zoom-on-cluster + direct camera positioning probe). Click opens Detail Panel with correct Claim. Click works across role switch + close/reopen cycles. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.5. Architecture spec gains §8.5 Phase 16.1.5 changelog bullet.
- 2026-05-10: **Phase 16.1.4 — Hotfix: Directory layer dot rendering regression from 16.1.3.** Single-issue hotfix. **Root cause**: the scene-init `useEffect` declared `phase` in its dependency array, so its cleanup fired on every internal phase transition (`opening → in`, `in → out`), not only on full close. The cleanup disposed the populated `InstancedMesh` + renderer; the body re-ran and created a fresh empty mesh (`count = 0`). The downstream `useLayoutEffect` that repopulates dots had deps `[threeReady, layout]` — those did not register a change because React batched the `setThreeReady(false)` in cleanup with the `setThreeReady(true)` in the new body into a single update whose net value was unchanged. **Fix**: derive a stable `shouldMountScene = phase !== 'closed'` boolean and depend on that instead of `phase`. The scene-init effect now runs setup only on the `closed → non-closed` boundary and cleanup only on `non-closed → closed`. Internal phase transitions no longer tear down and rebuild the scene. Verified across hard-reload + first open, 10 open/close cycles, 5 rapid-succession toggles in 3 seconds. Color variety + RFP cyan hollow circle + grey L-shape boundary + role-switching + wheel zoom all unaffected. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.4. Architecture spec gains §8.5 Phase 16.1.4 changelog bullet.

Combined with sync-render-after-attach, dots paint reliably on hard reload + rapid open/close cycles. **Item 2**: Actor square migrated from `LineSegments` to `Mesh` with hollow `ShapeGeometry`; border thickness scales with camera zoom. **Item 3**: squareCell reserved in cluster layout before umbrella/public/RFP placement; no more dot-on-square overlaps. **Item 4**: zoom controls top:73 (was 12) to clear the chrome bar. **Item 5**: Directory Detail Panel `bottom: 0 → 28` so the bottom button clears the footer. **Item 6**: click pans to center via `animatedPanToWithZoom`, mirroring V2Canvas's cubic-ease pattern with panel-aware offset. **Item 7**: Request EA modal close no longer dismisses Directory — removed `setV22DirectoryOpen(false)` + `setV22DirectorySelectedClaim(null)` from the EA-request callback. **Item 8**: disclosure-type-based dot colors (full→indigo, selective→amber, proof-only→green) replacing the visibility-scope scheme; seed types diversified for color variety; L-shape boundary changed to neutral grey. **Item 9**: RFP hollow circle in cyan (`--accent-cyan`), rendered as `Mesh` with hollow `ShapeGeometry` so the border scales with zoom. Footer rolls forward to v0.16.1.3. Architecture spec gains §8.5 Phase 16.1.3 changelog bullet.

- 2026-05-08: **Phase 16.1.0 — Three.js migration for Directory Layer.** Substantial sub-phase. - 2026-05-08: **Phase 16.0.2 — Hotfix sub-phase (PDF worker + duplicate-key + Phase 16.0 ship debt).** Three bugs surfaced after Phase 16.0 / 16.0.1 ship. No backlog items closed (these are pure regression / ship-debt fixes). **Item 1 (PDF.js worker, presentation-blocking)**: PDF previews failed with "Setting up fake worker failed" + 404 on `pdf.worker.mjs`. Root cause: when the dev server runs from the git worktree (`.claude/worktrees/...`) the worktree's `node_modules` is empty (only `.vite` cache); pdfjs-dist resolves to the parent repo's `node_modules` via Node's directory walk, but Vite's `?url` import then returns a `/@fs/<absolute>/...` URL that falls outside Vite's `server.fs.allow` security boundary. Fix path B from the brief (copy worker to `public/`): `cp /node_modules/pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs public/pdf.worker.mjs` + `pdfJsWorker.js` switched from `import workerSrc from 'pdfjs-dist/build/pdf.worker.mjs?url'` to `pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = '/pdf.worker.mjs'`. The static path is served directly from project root in dev AND production. Manual upgrade step documented inline: re-copy after `pdfjs-dist` updates. Build still bundles the worker normally because pdfjs-dist resolves through node's standard module path; only the runtime `workerSrc` URL is now static. **Item 2 (`v22DirectoryMaterializedClaim` ReferenceErrors)**: investigation confirmed the references were already fully cleared during the Phase 16.0 ship (the `sed` sweep caught all setter call sites + reader call sites — only a comment-level historical reference remains at line 241). The brief's report appears to have been based on a stale snapshot or HMR-cached transformation pre-dating the Phase 16.0 commit. Updated the Phase 11B-era "materialized directory Claim" comment block on the Detail Panel mount IIFE to reflect the Phase 16.0 per-dot click flow + `buildClaimNodeForDirectoryMaterialization` rename note. **Item 3 (duplicate React keys `0.10.0`, `0.9.10`)**: source identified — Changelog modal at `V2App.jsx:8502` iterates the changelog entries with `key={release.version}` alone, but multiple historical phases share the same version string (v0.10.0 covers 8 entries — Phase 10.1 through 11B; v0.9.10 covers Phase 9D.2.3 + 9D.2.4). Switched to composite key `${release.version}-${release.label}` since `label` (phase number) is unique per entry. Pre-existing bug — not introduced by Phase 16.0 — but surfaced by the user during 16.0 QA. **Footer remains v0.15.9** per established convention; v0.16.0.2 inserted into Changelog modal between v0.16.0.1 and v0.16.0.

Cell stride = 12px (CELL constant); max 6 cols (`MAX_COLS`); umbrella claims fill first so the amber subset occupies leading cells in row-major order. ChipCo's 7-amber + 7-public Bob view produces 6+6+2 rows with the umbrella subset spanning row 0 cols 0-5 + row 1 col 0. New `lShapePath(cells, anchorX, anchorY)` helper traces an SVG `<path>` outlining the umbrella subset — generalised to N rows with horizontal jogs at each row boundary where the next row is narrower. Path stroked at 1.5px `var(--accent-amber)` + filled at `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent-amber) 8%, transparent)`. The Phase 16.0 freeform `placeClusterDots` + `boundingRectFromDots` helpers retired. **Item 6**: `ActorSquare` rewritten — square shrunk from 40×40 to 6×6 (`ACTOR_SQUARE` constant), border 2px → 1.5px, `borderRadius` dropped (sharp corners). New `faded` prop drops opacity to 0.25 with a 150ms transition; `isHoverNearPillbox(hover, pillX, pillY)` performs an axis-aligned bbox intersection between the hovered Claim dot's footprint and the pillbox's PILLBOX_W × PILLBOX_H bounding box. ClaimDot hovered/pinned state now also paints a `0 0 8px rgba(255,255,255,0.6)` halo so the dot reads on top of any faded pillbox. **Item 7**: cluster horizontal positioning reworked — `pickClusterCenters` removed; new inline distribution in the `layout` useMemo: 1 cluster → x=50%, 2 clusters → x=35% / 65%, N≥3 → even spread `(i+1)/(N+1)`. All anchored at `y = viewport.h * 0.5`. Sort by `localeCompare` so MicroCo always lands left of ChipCo. **Item 8**: own RFP dots anchored to `(cardCenterX + offset, cardTopY - 24)` directly above Bob's corner card; multiple RFPs spread horizontally centered on the card. Other RFPs continue to anchor adjacent to their owning Actor's square. **Footer remains v0.15.9** (NOT bumped — same convention as 16.0); v0.16.0.1 inserted into Changelog modal between v0.16.0 and v0.15.9. Full `badgeTemplates` retained for `selectedTemplate` resolution + `handleNewVersion` lineage walk so id-based lookups still work. **Bug B**: `STEP_FAN` in `BadgeChipContainer.jsx` tuned 22 → 16 to compensate for the halo + SVG stroke geometry from Phase 14.5 — visible shield footprint exceeds `SHIELD_SIZE`, so the prior `SHIELD_SIZE + 4` math measured ~10px on screen. 16 lands the on-screen gap close to the 4px target. `STEP_IDLE`, `SHIELD_SIZE`, `HEIGHT` unchanged. **#184**: `evidence` subtype config entry purged from `CATEGORY_CONFIG` in `AssetNode.jsx`; graceful fallback via the existing `|| CATEGORY_CONFIG.product` pattern; V2Canvas `category === 'evidence'` filter retained as future-#50 cleanup material. **#183**: investigation surfaced heavy downstream chains for all four legacy-PDF Assets (`aEmiDatasheet` → `cEmi` + `prEmiDatasheet` + `daAlicePublic/Carol/Emi` + `erCarolEmi`; `dPrmIcDatasheet`/`dPrmIcTestReport` → ChipCo PRM-IC Assembly Claim + `daveOwnAssets` + ChipCo DAs; `dVrefDatasheet` → ChipCo voltage-reference Claim). Per the brief's "STOP and surface" rule, trim deferred to a per-Asset decision (path (a) generate branded PDFs vs path (b) cascade-trim — recommended (a) given the demo-narrative cost of (b)). #184 moved to Completed; #183 stays Open with the investigation captured. **Footer remains v0.15.9** (NOT rolled back); v0.14.6.1 inserted into Changelog modal in chronological order between v0.15.0 and v0.14.6.
