- 2026-05-07: **Phase 15.1 — Annotation Visual Redesign + Bidirectional Interaction (#172 part 2 of 3).** Five workstreams. **Step 0**: VReg re-run path verified — gated by `hasNewAssetsForRerun` until Alice amends Claim with a new Asset. Acceptable demo prerequisite per docs/PHASE-15-DEMO-SCENARIOS.md, no seed adjustment. **Step 1**: skipped per Step 0 outcome. **Step 2 (visual redesign)**: each anchor renders a translucent highlight rectangle (RS color, 15% opacity) over the cited text + a numbered indicator (26px circle, 12px mono bold) immediately to the LEFT of the rect. Highlighted state: rect bumps to 30%, indicator gains 3px ring outside its 2px white border. **Step 3 (side-by-side layout)**: Expand modal eval-output + poe schemas now render PDF + indicators on the left (~60%, sticky) + per-RS results tables on the right (~40%); falls back to vertical stack below 900px viewport. **Step 4 (bidirectional interaction)**: results-table rows gain a 22px numbered indicator on the left of each row. Lifted `highlightedAnchorId` state. Row click → highlight + scroll PDF + auto-flip Asset switcher if cross-Asset. Dot click → highlight + scroll matching row. Anchors are stable-keyed via new `src/v2/data/anchorIds.js` synthesizer (`{sourceAssetId}|{rsId}|{requirementId}|{page}|{x}|{y}`). **Step 4f (RunEval extension)**: ReviewRow gains the same indicator slot + click handler; cross-Asset clicks flip the accordion's expanded Asset. Implementation gotcha: row anchors must be re-enriched with `(requirementsSetId, requirementId)` at synthesis time since the seed anchor only carries `(sourceAssetId, page, x, y, w, h)`. **Footer v0.15.1 → v0.15.2.** Architecture spec §17.5 gains "Annotation visual model" + "Bidirectional interaction" subsections; Changelog gains a 15.1 bullet.
- 2026-05-06: **Phase 15.0.1 — Annotation Diagnosis + Layout Fixes + Git Catchup.** Five-deliverable phase. **Bug fix**: `makeEvaluationResult` factory in `src/v2/v2_2Data.js` was silently dropping `evidenceAnchors` from each result row's `results.map((r) => ({ … }))` projection. Phase 15.0 seed authoring populated `evidenceAnchors: [...]` on every row but the factory's explicit field-preserve list excluded it, so the consumer received zero anchors. Factory now preserves the field (cloned per-anchor for safety); all four target surfaces (eval flow, Eval Result expand Output, PoE expand Output, Re-Run flow) show dots correctly. **Layout**: PDF fit-to-width — AnnotatedPdfViewer derives the render scale from the host container's `clientWidth` at load time (capped at 1.6× base scale). Eliminates horizontal scroll inside narrow modal columns. **UX**: minimal multi-Asset switcher in the expand modal Output tab (Previous/Next arrow controls; arrows hide for single-Asset cases). Phase 15.1 will add auto-flip on dot click. **Doc**: new `docs/PHASE-15-DEMO-SCENARIOS.md` captures the four QA scenarios. **Git catchup**: 13 unpushed phases (12.4 → 15.0) pushed as two commits (`Catchup: Phases 12.4 → 14.5 (b → n)` + `Phase 15.0 — PDF.js Integration + Annotated Evidence (#172 part 1) — v0.15.0`). **Workflow lesson recorded**: runtime probes verify components, not integration. Phase 15.0 shipped with the viewer correctly verified standalone but never opened the actual user path (Detail Panel → Expand → Output) with seed data — which would have caught the factory bug immediately. Going forward, any phase touching a shared seed factory should add an explicit user-path walkthrough verification step before declaring complete. **Footer v0.15.0 → v0.15.1.**
- 2026-05-06: **Phase 15.0 — PDF.js Integration + Annotated Evidence Seed Content (#172 part 1 of 3).** XL phase. Foundation for the PDF annotation overlay feature. Three coupled deliverables: (1) seed-content authoring — three branded MicroCo PDFs (PRM-3A Datasheet, PRM-3A Test Report, VReg-12C Datasheet) generated from `scripts/generate-seed-pdfs.mjs`, a deterministic pdf-lib-based script that emits both the binary PDFs (to `public/seed-pdfs/`) AND a coordinate map (to `src/v2/data/evidenceAnchors.js`) in lockstep so seed and PDF positions never drift; (2) PDF.js infrastructure — `pdfjs-dist` runtime dep + worker config (`src/v2/components/pdfJsWorker.js`) using Vite's `?url` import; new `<AnnotatedPdfViewer>` component (`src/components/AnnotatedPdfViewer.jsx`) renders PDFs via canvas + overlays numbered dots at anchored coordinates with per-RS color coding; new `getRsColor`/`buildRsColorMap` helpers (`src/v2/data/rsColors.js`); (3) three integration surfaces — Run Evaluation modal Step 1 evidence panel, Eval Result expand modal Output tab, PoE expand modal Output tab — opt in via the new `usePdfJs` prop on `AssetEvidencePanel`; iframe rendering preserved everywhere else. Eval Result `results[]` rows gain `evidenceAnchors[]` arrays of `{ sourceAssetId, page, x, y, w, h }`. Demo scenario: Bob's PRM Eval Result references both PRM Datasheet and PRM Test Report Assets; MIL-PRF + System Integration RSes with anchors split across both PDFs. Static rendering only in 15.0 — Phase 15.1 will wire bidirectional row-click ↔ dot-click interaction; Phase 15.2 ships the walkthrough markdown + final polish. **Critical gotcha**: pdfjs-dist v5 changed the render API to use `canvas` as the primary parameter; the legacy `canvasContext` still paints but the returned promise never resolves — render task hangs. **Footer v0.14.5 → v0.15.0** (minor increment — substantial feature addition). Architecture spec gains a new §17.5 "Evidence Annotation" subsection covering scope, coordinate system, dot scheme, and forward-looking note on non-PDF evidence (CSVs, TXTs, images) per Andrew's flag.
- 2026-05-06: **Phase 14.5 — Chip Container Visual Tuning (#176c).** Six tuning corrections to Phase 14.4's chip container. (1) Each shield gains a 2px halo stroked in the rectangle's exact background color — produces the negative-space cut between adjacent overlapping shields that reads as the classic overlapping-tokens look. (2) Shields shrink 20px → 18px to fit the halo without growing the rectangle. (3) STEP_IDLE 15 → 12 for more pronounced ~33% overlap. (4) STEP_FAN 24 → 22 (= 18 + 4px gap; 4px gap unchanged). (5) Container height 26 → 24 (computed: `SHIELD_SIZE + PADDING * 2`). (6) Shields vertically centered against the rectangle midline via `top: (HEIGHT - SHIELD_SIZE) / 2`. (7) Single-shield case renders the rectangle as a 24×24 square with the lone shield horizontally + vertically centered — no fan-out branch (a single shield can't un-overlap). `BadgeShieldIcon` gained an optional `strokeColor` prop rendering a wider halo path beneath the silhouette path; `overflow: visible` on the SVG so the halo doesn't clip the viewBox. Container background, border, shadow, fan-out animation timing (180ms ease-out), and tooltip behavior unchanged. **Footer v0.14.4 → v0.14.5.** Architecture spec gains one Phase 14.5 changelog bullet; CLAUDE.md updated; phase log tail extended.
- 2026-05-05: **Phase 14.4 — Chip Container Visual Polish (#176b).** Single-purpose visual polish on Phase 14.3's `BadgeChipContainer`. Dropped the circular wrapper around each shield (was a 16px circle with `var(--bg-card)` background, 1.5px indigo border, shadow) — `BadgeShieldIcon` SVG now renders directly inside the chip rectangle. Dropped the pill wrapper around "+N" (was an 8px-radius indigo-tinted pill with its own border + shadow) — "+N" renders as plain `var(--font-mono)` 12px bold text in `var(--accent-indigo)`. Shields scaled 16px → 20px to fill the freed space; container height 22px → 26px (computed from `SHIELD_SIZE + PADDING * 2`). STEP values rescaled proportionally: STEP_IDLE 12 → 15 (75% of 20px → 25% overlap maintained), STEP_FAN 20 → 24 (20px + 4px gap → un-overlapped fan-out spacing maintained). Width math recomputed for the 5-badge demo case: idle 80px (was 72), hover 98px (was 88). Container background, border, shadow, fan-out animation timing (180ms ease-out), and tooltip behavior all unchanged. **Footer v0.14.3 → v0.14.4.** Architecture spec gains one Phase 14.4 changelog bullet; CLAUDE.md updated; phase log tail extended.
- 2026-05-05: **Phase 14.3 — Issue Badge Modal Scope Fix + Chip Container Refinement (#176a).** Two corrections to Phase 14.1 / 14.2's Badge work + a backlog filing. **Bug fix:** IssueBadgeModal picker scope. The Phase 14.1 sectioned picker rendered every actor's templates (My Badges + per-Actor sections); but Badge Templates can only be issued by their owner, so cross-actor templates were never legitimately issuance candidates. The picker now restricts strictly to the current actor's own templates as a single un-sectioned scroll list. Latest-version SUGGESTED auto-promotion within the user's own templates preserved. The unused `GlobeIcon` component + `others` / `otherByParty` state removed cleanly. **#176a — Chip container refactor:** Replaced the Phase 14.1 pattern of independent absolute-positioned chips + a separate adjacent "+N" pill with a single rounded-rectangle container. Visual treatment matches the NEW pill (4px border-radius, indigo-tinted background, subtle shadow). When 2+ shields are present, hover expands the rectangle leftward via 180ms ease-out; rightmost shield + "+N" stay anchored to the right edge; previously-overlapped shields slide left to un-overlap with 4px spacing. Per-shield tooltip reveals badge name + version (line 1) + issuer party (line 2). "+N" tooltip lists buried badges as `Badge Name · Issuer Party` rows. Both tooltips use the shared `<Tooltip>` primitive with auto-flip below the anchor when insufficient vertical space above. Click guard via `e.stopPropagation()` on the chip rectangle so clicks don't trigger card-level interactions. **Architecture:** New module `src/v2/BadgeChipContainer.jsx` (~190 lines) extracted from `AssetNode.jsx`. AssetNode's chip rendering block reduced to a single `<BadgeChipContainer>` mount with three props. The `_activeBadges` decoration in V2App's `v22DataWithReveal` enriched from raw badge-issuance objects to `{ id, badgeName, badgeVersion, issuerParty, badgeTemplateId }` shape so tooltips render without a second prop chain. Memo dep array gains `badgeTemplates`. **Backlog filing:** Netgraph cleanup item (#4 Layout density improvements) gains "Child-layer burial rules (Phase 14.3 design notes)" sub-section capturing burial candidates per node type + the Cascading Disclosures (#26) gating dependency. **Footer v0.14.2 → v0.14.3.** Architecture spec gains two Phase 14.3 changelog entries; CLAUDE.md "Current state of the world" + "Active phase queue" lines updated; phase log tail extended through Phase 14.3.

New notification type `v22-ea-amendment` (single-grantee, informational; deep-links to Claim + opens EA Detail Panel). EA Detail Panel: Amendments section between Status and paired-DA navigation; Amend footer button gated to grantor + active + non-revoked. Multi-EA implicit propagation + lineage chaining are documented limitations; Option C target deferred to production via new backlog item #160. Architecture spec §11.2a (new subsection) + §7.4 (notification table extended) + Changelog entry. #108 moved to Completed; #12 superseded note updated.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 13 — Proof of Evaluation (PoE) Node Type (#168) + #173 fold-in.** Major architectural addition: PoE introduced as a new first-class artifact type that wraps an active Eval Result batch (or solo Eval Result), terminates further evaluations on the same (Asset set, RS, evaluator) combination, and replaces individual Eval Results as the disclosure target for proof-only DAs. **Step 1 — `makePoE` factory** in `v2_2Data.js` with auto-generated name + structured DOT (hash null, like Claims) + metadata referencing wrapped Eval Result IDs. New `'poe'` added to `SUBJECT_KINDS`. **Step 2 — global rename `scope.evaluationResultIds` → `scope.poeIds`** across 6 files / ~20 touchpoints (v2_2Data.js, V2App.jsx, AmendDisclosureModal.jsx, CombinedResponseModal.jsx, DisclosureAgreementDetailPanel.jsx). No backwards-compat alias. **Step 3 — retroactive seed migration:** every existing seed Eval Result wraps into a PoE at seed construction time. Bob's two batch members share one PoE; Carol's solo wraps into its own. The Alice→Dave proof-only Claim DA's scope migrated to `poeIds: [poeBobPrm.id]`. The redundant `daProofBobPrmSysInt` proof-of-eval DA collapses into the single shared `daProofBobPrm`. `mergeProvisionals` extended with `proofsOfEvaluation` merge. **Step 4 — canvas adapter:** new `poeToNode` + `rollupPoeAggregate`; PoE column at `COL_OWN_POE = 2100`; counterparty PoEs hang next to source Claim. AssetNode renders `PROOF OF EVALUATION` type label + "Wraps N · X SAT · Y UNSAT" aggregate row. Edge derivation: `subject.kind === 'poe'` fans out wrapped Eval Result → Claim edges; proof-only Claim DA edges target PoE; new PoE↔EvalResult wrapping edges synthesized from artifact relationship. **Step 5 — entry points:** action-bar `◈` Create-PoE button + V22EvalResultPanel footer button, both gated on `isOwner && !isSuperseded && !node._alreadyWrapped`. V2App stamps `_alreadyWrapped: true` on Eval Result nodes already wrapped by an owned PoE. **Step 6 — `CreatePoEModal`:** confirmation modal with irreversibility warning + wrapped Eval Result list. **Step 7 — `V22PoEPanel`:** Owner / Source Claim (clickable) / Wrapped Eval Results (clickable rows + aggregate footer) / Disclosures (active DAs targeting this PoE) / Badges placeholder / DOT sections. Router wired with `case 'PROOF OF EVALUATION'`. **Step 8 — Run Eval submit-time gate:** new `existingPoEs` prop; for each selected RS, the modal blocks save when an owned PoE wraps that RS AND its `assetSnapshot` is a superset of the current evidence snapshot. Strict-superset check releases the gate when Alice amends evidence. Footer copy switches to amber with explanatory text. **Step 9 — disclosure flow:** AmendDisclosureModal proof-only branch + CombinedResponseModal proof-only step both rewritten to pick PoEs. Grantee canvas pull-in: `addPoeAndWrapped` helper pulls in both the PoE and its wrapped Eval Results; new `proofOnlyPulledPoeIds` set parallel to existing `proofOnlyPulledEvalIds`. **Step 10 — #173 fold-in:** collapsed accordion rows in Run Eval / Parse Evidence modals appeared to shrink when a sibling expanded because flex column's default `flex-shrink: 1` redistributed space. Fix: `flexShrink: 0` on each accordion card pins them to natural content height. **Documented deviations:** (1) Proof-of-eval DA edges fan out from wrapped Eval Results → Claim rather than emitting direct PoE → Claim edges (per design decision 14 — PoE node connects only to wrapped Eval Results). (2) PoE wrapping edges synthesized from a per-wrap synthetic internal Full DA for styling/dedupe consistency. (3) PoE column at 2100; counterparty PoEs at sourceClaim.x + 400. (4) Action-bar icon `◈` (diamond with cross). **Footer v0.12.7 → v0.13.0** (new minor for the architectural addition). Architecture spec gains Phase 13 entries. CLAUDE.md "Current state of the world" + "Active phase queue" updated with Phase 14 candidates (Badges #169, PDF annotation #172).
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.7 — Inline-Expand Asset Accordion (Scaling Fix) (#171c).** Pivoted Phase 12.6's split-container layout (Option B) to Option A: single overflow container holding rows with inline-expanded bodies. The 12.6 capped row list (`maxHeight: 40%`) didn't scale beyond ~5 in-scope Assets — at 10+ Assets the rows became too cramped to navigate. Inline expansion in natural flow scales to arbitrary Asset counts. **V22RunEvaluationModal + V22ParseEvidenceModal renderLeftPanel:** replaced the dual-container structure (capped row list + dedicated body card with `<AssetEvidencePanel fillHeight />`) with a single `flex: 1; minHeight: 0; overflowY: auto` container that holds each Asset as a self-contained card with an inline-expanded body. Card structure: outer `<div>` with accent-indigo / default border + `var(--bg-card)` background; inside, the row header (always rendered) followed by an optional body (rendered only when expanded). The body has `borderTop: 1px solid var(--border-faint)` + `padding: 10px 12px` + `var(--bg-surface)` background, holding `<AssetEvidencePanel assetRow={a} iframeHeight={480} />`. **iframe height 360 → 480:** the brief suggested ~480 as a reasonable replacement; with single-expand semantics only one body renders at a time so a more substantial preview is fine. **`fillHeight` retired from the modal accordions:** the prop made sense in 12.6's Option B body container (which used `flex: 1` to absorb height). With inline expansion in natural flow, the body sizes to its content — `fillHeight` no longer applies. The prop stays on `<AssetEvidencePanel>` so ExpandedArtifactModal's call (and any future caller) can opt in. **Parse Evidence height parity preserved:** the outer overflow container uses `flex: 1; minHeight: 0` so the column stretches to full available height regardless of content extent. When the inline card's natural height is shorter than the column, the scroll container fills the column with empty space below — visual height parity with the right panel preserved. **State / interaction unchanged:** single-expand semantics, `expandedAssetId: string|null`, default-first-expanded on mount, click-to-toggle, accent-indigo treatment on the expanded row. **No regressions:** Run Eval stage 2 sticky RS headers, stage 0 RS picker + diff banner, Parse Evidence three-tweak right panel — all untouched. **Footer v0.12.6 → v0.12.7.** Architecture spec gains a Phase 12.7 Changelog entry. CLAUDE.md "Active phase queue" updated to mark Phase 12 closed (truly this time); phase log tail extended through Phase 12.7.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.6 — Single-Expand Accordion + Panel Height Parity (#171b).** Follow-up to Phase 12.5 pivoting the Asset accordion from multi-expand to single-expand and structurally fixing the ~48px height shortfall on V22ParseEvidenceModal's left panel. **Single-expand state:** `expandedAssetIds: Set<assetId>` replaced by `expandedAssetId: string|null`. Initialized to first in-scope Asset on mount (or `null` for empty-evidence Claims). Toggle handler: clicking a row's header sets the expanded id to that row's id (collapsing whatever was previously expanded); clicking the currently-expanded row sets the value to `null` (full collapse). Phase 12.5's ref-based change-detection block that auto-expanded newly-arriving Assets removed entirely — newly-arriving Assets simply appear as additional row headers in the (now-scrollable) list. **Layout — Option B chosen:** the brief offered Option A (single overflow container holding rows + inline-expanded body) or Option B (separate row list + dedicated body container). Picked Option B because it lets the body container use `flex: 1; minHeight: 0` to absorb remaining column height — the structural fix for the height parity bug. Layout: `<FieldLabel>` + scrollable row-list container (`flexShrink: 0; maxHeight: 40%; overflowY: auto`) + dedicated body container (`flex: 1; minHeight: 0`). The body container holds `<AssetEvidencePanel assetRow={expandedRow} fillHeight />` which stretches via the new `fillHeight` prop. **AssetEvidencePanel `fillHeight` prop:** new optional boolean (default `false`). When `true`: outer container is `flex: 1; minHeight: 0; display: flex; flexDirection: column`; iframe / placeholder / Selective parsed-fields list use `flex: 1; minHeight: 200` instead of fixed `height: iframeHeight`; metadata header + footer + Selective Asset header keep `flexShrink: 0` so they hold natural height. ExpandedArtifactModal calls `<AssetEvidencePanel>` without the prop so it stays on the legacy fixed-height path (the modal's tab body region is sized by the modal). **V22RunEvaluationModal renderLeftPanel:** dropped the inline-body-inside-row pattern from 12.5; the row list and the body now live in separate sibling containers. Each row is just the header (Asset name + filename + disclosure-type tag + chevron). The expanded row gets an accent-indigo border + tinted background so it visually pairs with the body card below. **V22ParseEvidenceModal renderLeftPanel:** same Option B structure for visual consistency. The `parseAssetExpanded: bool` from 12.5 became `expandedAssetId: string|null` so the state shape matches Run Eval. The single-Asset case means the row stays expanded by default; toggling it collapses to header-only. **Height parity verified structurally:** before this phase the AssetEvidencePanel's iframe had a fixed `height: 360`, totaling ~530px including header + footer; the right panel's "FIELDS TO EXTRACT" panel used `flex: 1` to fill remaining height — leaving a ~48px gap. With `fillHeight`, the iframe now grows alongside the modal and both panels render at identical column heights regardless of viewport. The fix is structural (no magic numbers). **Visual choices:** (1) Row-list scroll cap at `maxHeight: 40%` (the brief allowed 40-50%; chose 40 to favor larger expanded body, the primary visual focus). (2) Body container styling: accent-indigo border + `var(--bg-card)` background outside, `var(--bg-surface)` background inside — visually links the expanded body to its accent-bordered row above. **Run Evaluation regression check:** stage 2 right-panel sticky headers (Phase 12.5) untouched; diff banner still scrolls naturally; disclosure-type branching in the expanded body unchanged; empty-state copy still gates submit. **Footer v0.12.5 → v0.12.6.** Architecture spec gains a Phase 12.6 Changelog entry. CLAUDE.md "Active phase queue" updated to mark Phase 12 closed; phase log tail extended through Phase 12.6.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.5 — Asset Accordion + Right-Panel Layout Fixes (#171a).** Follow-up to Phase 12.4. Converted V22RunEvaluationModal and V22ParseEvidenceModal left panels from the Phase 12.4 selector + viewer pattern to the V2.1 accordion pattern: each in-scope Asset is a row that expands inline to show `<AssetEvidencePanel>`. All Assets expanded by default at modal open (multi-evidence visibility is the feature's purpose); multiple rows can be expanded simultaneously and toggling one row doesn't affect others. Click anywhere on the row header (or Enter / Space when focused) toggles the body. Single-Asset Claims still use the accordion shape — one row, expanded by default. State management: `Set<assetId>` keyed by Asset id, with a ref-based change-detection block that auto-expands newly-arriving Assets if the in-scope set grows mid-review. **Run Evaluation right-panel unified scroll:** the Phase 12.3 per-RS scroll boxes (which didn't actually scroll because they sat inside the modal body's own scroll surface) replaced with a single overflow container at the right-panel root. Each RS group header uses `position: sticky; top: 0` and a solid `--bg-surface` background so it pins while its rows scroll past, then yields to the next group's header. Diff banner (#117) lives at the top of the same scroll surface and scrolls away naturally. Footer (aggregate counts + Back / Save) stays pinned at modal bottom outside the scroll container. **V22ParseEvidenceModal three right-panel tweaks:** (a) "Parsing extracts structured fields..." helper text moved from above the "Parse Template *" title to directly underneath it, before the template list. (b) Parse Template list retained at `maxHeight: 300px + overflowY: auto` (matches V22RunEvaluationModal's RS picker pattern). (c) "FIELDS TO EXTRACT" panel removed its 160px height cap and stretches via `flex: 1; minHeight: 0` to fill the available right-panel column height, matching the left-panel viewer's height for visual balance; internal scroll behavior unchanged. **Filed #172** — PDF annotation overlay (numbered evidence dots over the source PDF, bidirectional hover with review rows). Status `Open — end-of-Phase-12 assessment`; depends on #41 (PDF.js integration); not inserted into the strict Phase 13+ ordering yet — assessment criteria captured in the entry. **Footer v0.12.4 → v0.12.5.** Architecture spec gains a Phase 12.5 Changelog entry; CLAUDE.md "Active phase queue" line updated.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.4 — Asset File Viewer in Run Evaluation Modal (#171).** Restored the V2.1 Asset evidence viewer to V22RunEvaluationModal's left panel and applied the same split-panel parity to V22ParseEvidenceModal per spec §17.1. **Component lift:** Phase 11B's `AssetEvidenceViewer` + Phase 11D.2's Selective parsed-fields renderer (both inline inside `ExpandedArtifactModal.jsx` previously) extracted to a new `src/components/AssetEvidencePanel.jsx`. The new module exports a default `<AssetEvidencePanel assetRow>` that branches on `disclosureType` (`'full'` / `'owner'` → AssetEvidenceViewer with file metadata header + iframe + footer; `'selective'` → parsed-fields table; `'proofonly'` → defensive empty-state error message). Also exports the named `AssetEvidenceViewer` and `SelectiveDisclosurePanel`. ExpandedArtifactModal refactored to consume the lifted components — single source of truth across all three modal surfaces. **V22RunEvaluationModal:** Modal width unified to 1280px across all three steps (was 720 / 920 jumping between stages). 1:1 grid + 24px gutter + vertical divider between columns. Left panel renders an Asset selector list (max-height 180px scrollable) + the disclosure-type-aware `<AssetEvidencePanel>` filling remaining height. First in-scope Asset preselected; clicking a selector row swaps the viewer body without resetting review state on the right. Selector rows show name + filename + a small disclosure-type tag (Owner / Full / Selective). Stage 2 lost its legacy 260px Asset list — replaced by the new selector + viewer in the 1:1 left half; right column is now solely the grouped review rows + diff banner. Stages 0/1/2 all use the same split layout. Empty-evidence Claims render an empty-state message in the left panel mirroring the right-panel copy from #105. **V22ParseEvidenceModal:** Same 1280px modal width + 1:1 grid + same divider treatment. Selector list collapses to a single static row (Parse is owner-only, single Asset per §10.2). Viewer always renders AssetEvidenceViewer (`disclosureType: 'owner'`). Right panel content unchanged. Legacy 620px stage 0/1 modal replaced — all three stages render at 1280px so width doesn't jump between steps. **V2App `evidenceAssets` enrichment:** prop shape grew from `{ id, name, file }` to `{ id, name, file, asset, disclosureType, disclosedFields? }`. For self-eval `disclosureType` = `'owner'`; for non-self the active DA's `type` drives the row; `'selective'` resolves disclosed fields by intersecting `da.scope.fieldIds` against the Asset's Parse Result fields, identical to Phase 11D.2's Detail Panel pattern. V22ParseEvidenceModal's `sourceAsset` prop also got `file` + `registrationDate` (resolved from the Asset card node's `v22Artifact`). **Scope boundaries:** real PDF.js integration still tracked as #41; backfilling more placeholder PDFs deferred (the "Document preview not available" fallback handles missing files cleanly); Eval Result / Parse Result file viewing in their own Detail Panels deferred separately; UI-only phase (no data model changes); `<AssetEvidencePanel>` placed in `src/components/` alongside `Tooltip.jsx` rather than a new `src/v2/components/` directory. **Footer v0.12.3 → v0.12.4.** Architecture spec §8.7 updated to note the lifted shared components; Changelog gains a Phase 12.4 entry. CLAUDE.md "Current state of the world" + "Active phase queue" lines updated; phase log tail extended through Phase 12.4. #171 filed and moved straight to Completed in this hygiene pass.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.3 — Multi-RS UX Refinements + Asset Removal Bug Fix.** Three bug fixes + two UX pivots patching Phase 12.2's evaluation flow against QA findings. **Bug A — RS picker dedup:** the modal now accepts a new `publicRequirementSets` prop alongside the existing `availableRequirementsSets`. Composition merges by id with owner-authored winning on duplicates so the row's provenance badge reads "Authored by you" rather than "Public" when the same RS is reachable through both pools. The modal previously only saw owner-authored RS; public RS now surface in the picker too. **Bug B — Asset Remove consumer audit:** Phase 12.2's `assetReferences[]` chain correctly derives the post-amend active `referencedAssetIds[]`, but two consumers were reading stale primitives: (1) `deriveAgreementEdges` walked internal claim-ref DAs' `scope.assetIds` without intersecting against the Claim's current active set — Removed Assets' edges persisted on the canvas. (2) Run Evaluation's `scopeAssetIds` for non-self path read `da.scope.assetIds` (which doesn't auto-update when Alice amends her Claim). Both fixed by intersecting against `claim.referencedAssetIds`. New `getInScopeAssets(claim, allAssets)` helper added to `v2_2Data.js` as the single source of truth for "what Assets does this Claim reference?"; chain-aware (resolves `supersededBy`, filters `removedDate`). #170 filed for the broader `assetReferences[]` / `referencedAssetIds[]` consolidation as a future tech-debt phase. **Bug C — Supersession Detail Panel link:** the V22EvalResultPanel "Supersession" section's list item is now clickable using the same row-click pattern as the Sibling Evaluations section; click navigates to the successor Eval Result's Detail Panel. V2App's `siblingEvalResults` prop extended to also include the `supersededBy` successor when present (re-runs typically generate a new batchId so the successor isn't otherwise in the batch lookup). **Pivot 1 — Checkbox multi-select picker:** replaced primary/additional split + `+ BATCH` chip with a uniform checkbox list. All checked RSes treated equally; submit produces one Eval Result per checked RS sharing one batch id. No primary/additional concept at runtime. Locked Re-Evaluate flow auto-checks the locked RS and disables every other row. State refactor: `selectedReqSetId` (single) + `additionalReqSetIds` (array) replaced by `selectedReqSetIds` (ordered array). `rows` (single array) replaced by `rowsByRsId` (object map) with per-RS row mutators (`cycleStatus(rsId, idx, dir)`, `updateValue(rsId, idx, value)`). **Pivot 2 — Grouped review rows:** Review stage renders one section per checked RS, in check order. Each section: header band (RS name + version + requirement count) + per-requirement rows. Submit-with-defaults still works — AI-suggested values flow through unchanged for any Set Bob doesn't curate. Footer SAT/UNSAT/MISSING/N/A summary aggregates across all selected RSes. The Phase 12.2 diff banner (#117) still surfaces above the grouped sections. **Documented deviations:** (1) Submit-time duplicate detection now skips the §11 supersession-style block when 2+ RSes are checked — running the same evidence against multiple RSes is the explicit feature of the multi-RS flow. Single-RS submits still get the dup-detection UX. (2) Visual-density "collapse-by-default when N>2" pattern from the brief was deferred — flat-rendered grouped sections shipped instead; collapse-by-default can be filed as a polish item later. **Footer v0.12.2 → v0.12.3.** Architecture spec gains five Phase 12.3 changelog entries; new helper `getInScopeAssets` documented in §10.3b. CLAUDE.md "Active phase queue" line updated.
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.2 — Evaluation Architecture Cluster (#106, #117, #121, #122, #105) + backlog hygiene + new item filings (#168 PoE, #169 Badges).** Five evaluation items shipped together; major restructure of the Run Evaluation flow + new Asset versioning + OUTDATED Eval Result lifecycle. **#122 Asset versioning:** Claims gain a parallel `assetReferences[]` audit chain on top of the existing primitive `referencedAssetIds[]` — entries `{ assetId, supersededBy, addedDate, removedDate }`. Documented deviation: kept `referencedAssetIds` as primitives + added the chain in parallel rather than converting all 42+ consumer call sites; the brief explicitly allowed this either-or path. AmendClaim grew Replace + Remove affordances per evaluated-Asset row — Replace opens a successor picker that stamps `supersededBy` on the existing entry and appends a fresh entry for the replacement; Remove stamps `removedDate` on the existing entry without successor. Asset node itself is NEVER modified. New Eval Result `status` value `'outdated'` triggered when any `evidenceUsed` Asset has been superseded or removed since the evaluation. New helpers `isEvalResultStale`, `getLatestAssetVersion`. AmendClaim submit fires `v22-eval-result-stale` notifications (single-grantee, informational, OUTDATED badge label, click deep-links to the Eval Result) and flips status synchronously. AssetNode renders an OUTDATED badge in Row 0 + dashed amber card border. V22EvalResultPanel renders an "Out of date" notice section near the top with re-run guidance. **#106 Drop Asset picker:** Run Evaluation modal opens directly to a review-rows surface; `evidenceUsed` is computed at submit time as the snapshot of all currently-in-scope Assets. Submit gates simplified — empty-evidence Claims block via the new role-split copy path. **#121 Multi-RS:** RS picker grew a primary/additional split (primary radio-like, additional `+ BATCH` chip toggle); submit produces N Eval Results sharing a freshly-generated `batchId`. Solo evaluations carry `batchId: null`. `makeEvaluationResult` extended with `batchId`, `priorEvalResultId`, `evidenceDiff` fields. V22EvalResultPanel renders a "Sibling Evaluations" section listing batch members when `batchId !== null` and the batch contains 2+ members; section omitted otherwise (backwards-compat for pre-12.2 seed Eval Results). **#117 Re-run diff:** new helper `computeEvidenceDiff(priorEvalResult, claim)` returns `{ added, removed, superseded, carried }`. Run Evaluation modal renders a "Changes since last evaluation" banner above review rows when a non-empty diff exists. V22EvalResultPanel renders a "Changes from prior evaluation" section persisting the diff in audit trail. Re-run flips prior Eval Result status to `'superseded'` with `supersededBy` set to the new id. **#105 Empty-state copy:** owner-specific vs. non-owner-specific copy split, gated via new `isOwnerView` prop. **Demo data:** Bob's existing PRM Eval Result retro-fitted with `batchId: 'batch-seed-bob-prm-001'`; new sibling Eval Result `eval-bob-prm-002` (System Integration v1) ships in the same batch + new proof / ownership DAs so both batch members appear on Alice's canvas. OUTDATED scenario reachable in 2 clicks via AmendClaim → Replace/Remove on an evaluated row. **Backlog hygiene:** 6 items moved to Completed (#9, #16, #18, #19, #81, #110); 4 items moved to a new `## Removed` section with audit-trail rationale (#31, #32, #46, #114); #72 rewritten with narrowed PoE-transfer scope; #168 (Proof of Evaluation node type) and #169 (Badges) filed in Future Features as Phase 13+ priorities #1 + #2. **Documentation:** spec §10.3 + new §10.3a updated for the Asset versioning chain + OUTDATED lifecycle; spec Changelog gains five Phase 12.2 entries (one per item). CLAUDE.md "Active phase queue" line updated with Phase 13+ strict-ordered priorities. **Footer v0.12.1 → v0.12.2.**
- 2026-05-04: **Phase 12.1 — Reference Published Requirements Sets on a Claim (#120).** Single-item phase shipping non-binding "Referenced Standards" metadata on Claims as a discoverability signal — strictly informational, no evaluation coupling, no notifications. **Data model:** `makeClaim` factory in `v2_2Data.js` gains `referencedRequirementsSets: [{ requirementsSetId, addedDate }]`. Each `amendments[]` entry gains parallel `addedRequirementsSetIds[]` / `removedRequirementsSetIds[]` arrays. Both fields version-pinned (store the specific RS version id, not a lineage / "latest" pointer). New helper `getLatestRSVersion(rsId, allRS)` walks the RS supersession chain and returns the latest version's id. `makeAmendedClaim` extended to accept the RS diff buckets; cascade-skip semantics are inherited from the existing handler (no Eval Result staleness, no notification fan-out). **Picker primitive:** new `RequirementsSetPicker` shared component with two-tab pool (My Requirements Sets / Published), search, locked-row support for already-referenced entries. Used by `V22CreateClaimModal` (optional create-time section) and `AmendClaimModal` (toggleable add picker). **Amend Claim modal:** restructured from Asset-add-only to support both Asset add and RS add/remove. Submit shape now `{ addedAssetIds, addedRequirementsSetIds, removedRequirementsSetIds }`; gated on at-least-one of the three buckets. Existing references render with × buttons + Undo on toggle; new picks render with NEW badges in an inline preview. **Inline supersession update:** new `UpdateRSReferenceModal` opens exclusively from the Detail Panel "Newer version available" pill (owner-only click). Confirm records a one-line amendment with diff = removed `[oldVersionId]`, added `[latestVersionId]` and updates the array entry's `requirementsSetId` to the latest version with a fresh `addedDate`. Update jumps to the latest in the supersession chain (not the next link). The only inline mutation affordance on this section — precedent does not extend elsewhere. **Detail Panel:** `V22ClaimPanel` Referenced Standards section between Referenced Assets and Acknowledgments. Each row: clickable name (deep-link to Library at the originally-referenced version, NOT the latest, with provenance-aware tab routing — own → Requirement Sets, public → Published), provenance badge ("Authored by you" / "Public"), optional "Newer version available" amber pill (owner click → UpdateRSReferenceModal; non-owner → static informational text), `addedDate` in muted small text. Empty state omits the section. **Demo data:** Alice's `claim-prm-assembly` references MIL-PRF-55681 v1 (public, supersession case) + Incoming QC v1 (authored). `claim-vreg-ic` references System Integration v1 (public). Dave's `claim-chipco-prm-ic` references MIL-PRF-55681 v2 (public, latest — exercises Public badge without the supersession pill). New v2 of MIL-PRF-55681 added to bob-govco's authored RS list adding an EMI/EMC requirement on top of v1's six rows. **Scope deviations:** (1) Brief mentioned an `ExpandedArtifactModal` mirror — no Claim expanded view exists in V2.2 (Detail Panel V22ClaimPanel IS the canonical expanded view), so the section renders only there. (2) Brief mentioned "one Claim per demo role" with references — only Alice and Dave own Claims in the demo (Bob and Carol are evaluators, not Claim owners), so seeded references span Alice + Dave; cross-role coverage is achieved via grantee canvases when Bob/Carol have a DA on Alice's/Dave's referenced Claims (referencedRequirementsSets travels with the Claim artifact). (3) AmendClaim diff render is inline (existing rows with strikethrough on removal-marked, NEW-badged preview on additions) rather than a separate two-step confirmation flow — keeps the existing single-step modal pattern; the diff signal is preserved without restructuring the surface. **Footer v0.11.31 → v0.12.1** (first phase of the 12.x series). - 2026-05-03: **Phase 11.6.1 — Five fixes from Phase 11.6 QA.** **Fix 1:** `handleV22AmendmentAccept` and `handleV22AmendmentReject` now dismiss the originating `v22-ea-amendment-proposal` notification on the grantee's inbox after a terminal response. The proposal notification id is deterministic (`v22-ea-amendment-proposal-${eaId}-${amendmentId}`), so both handlers append it to `dismissedReqs` via `updateRoleState(roleId, ...)` immediately after `setV22RespondingToEaAmendment(null)`. Pre-fix the notification persisted indefinitely. **Fix 2:** `EvaluationAgreementDetailPanel` now renders an Acknowledgments section sourced from the live `claim.acknowledgments[]` (passed as a new `claim` prop from V2App). Spec §11.2a Option B says acknowledgments live on the Claim, not the EA — the EA's `acknowledgmentsAccepted` is an audit-trail snapshot of original acceptance time, not the live state. Pre-fix the panel had no Acknowledgments section at all, so post-amendment changes were invisible until the user opened the Claim Detail Panel directly. **Fix 3:** Run Evaluation button on the Claim Detail Panel now stays visible during `pending-acceptance` (visually disabled with tooltip directing grantee to respond), instead of falling through to "Request Evaluation Agreement." V2App's `evaluationAgreementForActor` resolver extended via `isActionableEaStatus = (s) => s === 'active' \|\| s === 'pending-acceptance'`; V22ClaimPanel's footer renders Run Evaluation as `<FooterButton disabled title="Cannot run evaluation: amendment proposal awaiting your response. Respond in your inbox to continue.">` when the EA's status is pending-acceptance. **Fix 4:** EA Detail Panel footer's `canRevoke` predicate extended to allow revocation during pending-acceptance per spec §11.2b — revocation is the documented override during pending. The pending amendment record stays in `amendments[]` for audit; the EA goes to `revoked` regardless. **Fix 5:** Both `AmendEvaluationAgreementModal` (grantor's view) and `AmendmentResponseModal` (grantee's view) now display a "Current terms" section at the top of the modal body — read-only display of the EA's pre-amendment expiration + the Claim's pre-amendment acknowledgments — followed by a divider, then the existing editable / diff-display sections (relabeled "Proposed expiration" / "Proposed acknowledgments" / "Proposed acknowledgment changes" / "Proposed expiration change" so the contrast is explicit). Andrew's note: "keep it simple — there will be a big redesign of all modal flows later on." No spec changes. Footer v0.11.28 → v0.11.29.
